### [112] [Hecke operators on symplectic surfaces and $χ$-independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01355)
*Ben Davison,Lucien Hennecart,Tasuki Kinjo,Olivier Schiffmann,Eric Vasserot*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于拟射影辛曲面上一维层模空间的BPS上同调的Toda χ-独立性猜想，并确定了与一维Mukai向量相关的BPS李代数与示性类子空间的关系，将Markman的示性类生成定理从原始Mukai向量推广到任意Mukai向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Toda提出的χ-独立性猜想，并将Markman的示性类生成定理从原始Mukai向量推广到任意情形。

Method: 通过构建拟射影辛簇S上凝聚层的上同调Hall代数的双代数结构，利用三维上同调Hall代数的因式分解余积通过维数约化得到余积，进而构造全局BPS李代数；利用Hecke算子将一维层通过零维商修正，建立BPS上同调与余积原始部分的等同关系。

Result: 证明了Toda χ-独立性猜想；识别了BPS李代数与示性类子空间；将Markman定理推广到任意Mukai向量。

Conclusion: 建立了双代数结构与Hecke算子之间的联系，为理解模空间上同调提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We prove Toda's chi-independence conjecture for the BPS cohomology of moduli spaces of one-dimensional sheaves on quasi-projective symplectic surfaces, relative to the Chow variety. We also identify the BPS Lie algebra associated with one-dimensional Mukai vectors with the subspace of tautological classes, giving an extension of Markman's tautological generation theorem from primitive to arbitrary Mukai vectors. The main structure input is a bialgebra structure on the cohomological Hall algebra of coherent sheaves on a quasi-projective symplectic variety S. The coproduct is obtained, by dimensional reduction, from a factorization coproduct for 3d cohomological Hall algebras, and gives rise to a global BPS Lie algebra attached to the stack of coherent sheaves on S. The link between this structure and the applications to chi-independence and tautological generation is provided by Hecke operators on BPS cohomology, which modify one-dimensional sheaves by zero-dimensional quotients. To make this construction work, we prove that there is an identification between the affinized BPS cohomology of the semistable locus and the primitive part of the coproduct on the entire moduli stack

</details>


### [113] [Interpolation for rational curves with secants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01432)
*Alessio Cela,Carl Lian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在任意特征域下，确定了射影空间 $\mathbb{P}^r$ 中满足沿线性空间附加割线条件的 $d$ 次有理曲线通过一般点的最大数目，并分别对应了 $\mathsf{Bl}_{\mathbb{P}^s}\mathbb{P}^r$ 中一般有理曲线的法丛和限制切丛的确定问题。附录中枚举了曲线上点被指定时的插值曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在任意特征域下，带有附加割线条件（沿线性空间）的有理曲线通过一般点的最大数目问题，并厘清该问题与法丛和限制切丛的对应关系。

Method: 通过将问题转化为 $\mathsf{Bl}_{\mathbb{P}^s}\mathbb{P}^r$ 中一般有理曲线的法丛（点未指定情形）和限制切丛（点指定情形）的确定问题，并在附录中枚举了指定点情形下的插值曲线。

Result: 确定了在任意特征域下，满足沿线性空间割线条件的 $d$ 次有理曲线通过一般点的最大数目。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了经典插值问题的结论，并建立了与 blow-up 空间上向量丛的紧密联系。

Abstract: In arbitrary characteristic, we determine the maximum number of general points through which a rational curve of degree $d$ in $\mathbb{P}^r$ passes, subject to an additional secancy condition along a linear space. We consider the cases both where the points on the curve are unprescribed and prescribed, which amount to the determination of the normal and restricted tangent bundles of a general rational curve in $\mathsf{Bl}_{\mathbb{P}^s}\mathbb{P}^r$, respectively. In the appendix, we enumerate the interpolating curves in the case of prescribed points on the curve.

</details>


### [114] [$χ$-independence for K3-surfaces via $p$-adic integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01513)
*Michael Groechenig,Dimitri Wyss,Paul Ziegler*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过归约到非阿基米德局部域，证明了一个此前未知的Toda χ-独立性猜想案例。策略基于比较2-Calabi-Yau范畴中对象模空间上BPS层的Frobenius迹与障碍gerbe的复指数Hasse不变量的积分。该结果适用于Nakajima箭图簇、Higgs丛模空间和K3曲面上的层模空间等重要情形。在此过程中，描述了这些模叠和模空间在混合特征大基上的局部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Toda χ-独立性猜想的一个先前未知的案例，该猜想涉及模空间上的数值不变量在不同稳定性条件或参数下的不变性。

Method: 通过归约到非阿基米德局部域，比较2- Calabi-Yau范畴中对象模空间上BPS层的Frobenius迹与障碍gerbe的复指数Hasse不变量积分。

Result: 证明了Toda χ-独立性猜想的一个此前未知的案例，并描述了相关模叠和模空间在混合特征大基上的局部结构。

Conclusion: 该结果适用于Nakajima箭图簇、Higgs丛模空间和K3曲面上的层模空间等重要情形，为相关领域提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: This article provides a proof of a previously unknown case of Toda's $χ$-independence conjecture by reduction to non-archimedean local fields. Our strategy is based on a novel comparison of Frobenius-traces for BPS sheaves on moduli spaces of objects in 2-Calabi-Yau categories and the integral of the complex-exponentiated Hasse invariant of the obstruction gerbe. This result applies to many cases of interest, including Nakajima quiver varieties, moduli of Higgs bundles and moduli of sheaves on K3 surfaces. Along the way, we describe the local structure of these moduli stacks and spaces over a base of large mixed characteristic.

</details>


### [115] [A note on cubical Bloch--Levine cycle complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01540)
*Peter Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文验证了Levine的单纯-立方比较论证在任意离散赋值环上同样成立，从而证明了立方Bloch圈复形层在Dedekind基上的光滑概形上可以计算动机上同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Levine关于Bloch圈复形的单纯-立方比较论证从特定情形推广到任意离散赋值环（DVR）上，从而扩展动机上同调计算工具的适用范围。

Method: 检验Levine的单纯-立方比较论证在任意DVR上的有效性，通过分析该论证对底环的依赖性，证明其可以脱离原有限制条件。

Result: Levine的比较论证在任意DVR上成立，进而立方Bloch圈复形层在Dedekind基上的光滑概形上可计算动机上同调。

Conclusion: 立方Bloch圈复形在更广泛的底环（Dedekind基）上仍然具有计算动机上同调的功能，推广了已有结果。

Abstract: We check that Levine's simplicial--cubical comparison argument for Bloch's cycle complexes also works over an arbitrary DVR. As a result, the sheaf of cubical Bloch cycle complexes computes motivic cohomology for smooth schemes over Dedekind bases.

</details>


### [116] [Foliated and Mather-Jacobian discrepancies via tangential arcs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01809)
*Maurício Corrêa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对三维簇上对数简单余秩一叶状结构，发展了一种切向弧空间方法，研究了相对于固定不变正规交叉可分奇点除子的叶状差异。在非共振对数情形下，证明了以规定切向轨迹为中心的约化切向弧被限制在该除子上。切向扇区在约化弧层面上由正规化的可分奇点-导体系统表示。叶状邻接将差异计算转移至正规化分支和导体上的普通邻接对。应用Ein-Mustaţă-Yasuda的弧空间定理，得到了切向余维数公式，将环面切向除子簇的对数余维数与相应的切向差异等同起来。由此建立了环面切向邻接反转、切向非lc和非klt轨迹的分支-导体描述、切向对数规范性的柱面判据、环面切向最小对数差异的下半连续性，以及典范像可分奇点系统的相对Mather-Jacobian精细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为三维簇上对数简单余秩一叶状结构建立系统的切向差异理论，填补该领域在弧空间方法方面的空白，并发展切向邻接反转等关键工具。

Method: 采用切向弧空间方法，结合叶状邻接技术，将叶状差异计算转化为正规化分支和导体上的普通邻接对问题，并应用Ein-Mustaţă-Yasuda弧空间定理导出切向余维数公式。

Result: 建立了切向余维数公式、环面切向邻接反转定理、切向非lc和非klt轨迹的分支-导体描述、切向对数规范性的柱面判据、环面切向最小对数差异的下半连续性，以及相对Mather-Jacobian精细化。

Conclusion: 本文发展的切向弧空间方法为三维簇上对数叶状结构的奇点分析提供了系统框架，所得一系列结果（如邻接反转、下半连续性等）是叶状结构奇点理论的重要进展。

Abstract: This article develops a tangential arc-space approach to foliated discrepancies for logarithmic simple co-rank one foliations on threefolds, relative to a fixed invariant normal-crossing separatrix divisor. In the non-resonant logarithmic case, reduced tangential arcs centred on the prescribed tangential locus are shown to be confined to this divisor. The tangential sector is therefore represented, at the reduced arc level, by the normalised separatrix-conductor system. Foliated adjunction transfers the discrepancy calculus to ordinary adjunction pairs on the normalised branches and conductors. Applying the arc-space theorem of Ein-Mustaţă--Yasuda on these strata, this yields a tangential codimension formula identifying logarithmic codimensions of toroidal tangential divisorial cylinders with the corresponding tangential discrepancies. The resulting theory gives a toroidal tangential inversion of adjunction, a branch--conductor description of the tangential non-lc and non-klt loci, a cylinder criterion for tangential log canonicity, lower semicontinuity of the toroidal tangential minimal log discrepancy, and a relative Mather--Jacobian refinement for the canonical image separatrix system.

</details>


### [117] [Motivic Hochschild homology of mod 2 motivic cohomology over algebraically closed fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01862)
*Tom Bachmann,Robert Burklund,Bjørn Ian Dundas,Paul Arne Østvær*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文计算了复数域上乘法群模式与模2 motivic上同调谱的张量，发现得到的代数是自由代数，生成元在双次数(2,1)上，给出了Bökstedt周期性的motivic类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Bökstedt周期性的motivic类比，理解模2 motivic上同调谱在乘法群模式张量下的代数结构。

Method: 通过比较tau-逆和tau-约化的张量形式：tau-逆情形借助Betti实现归约为经典Bökstedt周期性；tau-约化情形比较normed代数结构与导出代数结构，引入分次幂运算，并分析motivic扩展幂及tau-挠结构。

Result: 张量代数在双次数(2,1)上自由生成；与Dundas-Hill-Ormsby-Øvstær的单纯圆计算相比，Gm-张量下tau-挠类大量消失，代数结构更为刚性；得到了混合Cartan和Adem关系。

Conclusion: 成功建立了Bökstedt周期性的motivic类比，揭示了motivic设置下乘法群模式张量与经典Bökstedt周期性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We compute the tensor of the multiplicative group scheme with the mod-$2$ motivic cohomology spectrum in normed motivic spectra over the complex numbers, and find that the resulting algebra is free on a generator in bidegree (2,1). This gives a motivic analog of Bökstedt periodicity.
  The proof proceeds by comparing the tau-inverted and tau-reduced forms of the tensor. After inverting tau, the calculation reduces to classical B{ö}kstedt periodicity via Betti realization. The reduction modulo tau is governed by a comparison between normed algebra structures and derived algebra structures on cellular modules over motivic cohomology mod tau. This comparison produces divided power operations and leads to mixed Cartan and Adem relations intertwining normed and topological power operations.
  A key input is a detailed analysis of motivic extended powers of spheres and their tau-torsion structure. In contrast with the corresponding simplicial-circle calculation due to Dundas-Hill-Ormsby-Østvær, the large families of tau-torsion classes disappear for the Gm-tensor, leaving a considerably more rigid algebraic structure.

</details>


### [118] [Petersen graph and monodromy of the 27 lines on the Clebsch surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01878)
*Tathagata Basak*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文通过Petersen图显式描述了光滑三次曲面模空间基本群的单值作用，该作用生成Weyl群E₆，并给出了10个显式生成元的具体计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典已知光滑三次曲面模空间的基本群在27条直线上的单值作用生成Weyl群E₆，但缺乏该作用的显式描述。本文旨在通过Petersen图给出该作用的显式生成元计算，以便更直观地理解这一经典结果。

Method: 以Clebsch曲面为基点，利用与Allcock和Looijenga合作得到的10个显式生成元，通过初等计算在Petersen图上具体实现这些生成元对27条直线的置换作用。

Result: 成功给出了光滑三次曲面模空间基本群在27条直线上单值作用的显式描述，将其转化为Petersen图上的具体置换操作。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解Weyl群E₆在Petersen图上的作用提供了一个具体而直观的框架，也有助于深化对模空间作为复4-球商空间中判别式补集的理解。

Abstract: Let $G$ be the orbifold fundamental group of the moduli space of smooth cubic surfaces $\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{sm}}$ in $\mathbb{P}^3_{\mathbb{C}}$ with base point at the Clebsch surface $X_{\mathbf{1}}$. The image of the monodromy action $G \to \lbrace \text{Permutations of $27$ lines on $X_{\mathbf{1}}$} \rbrace$ is famously the Weyl group of type $E_6$. Here we give a description of this monodromy action in terms of the Petersen graph by working out the action of ten explicit generators of $G$ by elementary calculation. These ten generators were found in joint work with Allcock and Looijenga while studying the description of $\mathcal{M}_{\mathsf{sm}}$ as a discriminant complement in a complex $4$-ball quotient.

</details>


### [119] [A nine-line counterexample to a conjecture on the minimal degree of Jacobian relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01985)
*Alexandru Dimca,Piotr Pokora*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在复射影平面中构造出两组九线排列，它们具有同构的交格，但雅可比关系的最小次数不同（分别为4和5），从而为广义Terao猜想提供了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找广义Terao猜想的反例，该猜想认为线排列的(弱)组合结构决定其自由性。已知Ziegler-Yuzvinsky对（18线，6个三重交点）是经典反例，作者希望构造一个弱组合数据不同的新反例。

Method: 构造两个九线排列f和g，具有相同的交格（弱组合数据为(n₂,n₃,n₄)=(9,7,1)），分别计算它们的雅可比关系最小次数mdr(f)=4和mdr(g)=5。

Result: 得到两个九线排列，mdr(f)=4 < d/2=4.5，而mdr(g)=5。由于mdr(f) < d/2，该排列不自由，而相同组合数据的另一个排列mdr(g)=5 > d/2，可能自由，说明自由性不由组合结构唯一决定。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了广义Terao猜想的一个新反例，其弱组合数据为(9,7,1)，不同于经典的Ziegler-Yuzvinsky对的(18,6)，表明即使弱组合数据更简单，自由性仍不由组合结构决定。

Abstract: We construct two arrangements of nine lines in the complex projective plane with isomorphic intersection lattices but with different minimal degrees of Jacobian relations. The common weak combinatorics is \[ (n_2,n_3,n_4)=(9,7,1), \] so the example is not the classical Ziegler-Yuzvinsky pair, whose weak combinatorics is $(n_{2},n_{3}) = (18,6)$. For the two defining equations $f$ and $g$ we prove \[ {\rm mdr}(f)=4,\qquad {\rm mdr}(g)=5. \] Since the degree is $d=9$, the first equality gives ${\rm mdr}(f)<d/2$. Hence the pair gives a counterexample to the Generalized Terao Conjecture.

</details>


### [120] [Comb smoothing and local triviality of homogeneous spaces over a relative curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02014)
*Margot Bruneaux,Federico Scavia*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Hensel局部环上光滑射影曲线的G-扭局部平凡性，证明了在某些条件下（如中心同源核是étale的或剩余域足够大），纤维在特殊纤维上的Zariski局部平凡性可提升到整体曲线上，并得到了扭子的局部-整体原理、Drinfeld-Simpson定理的Hensel版本以及Brauer-Azumaya群单射性等推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将代数曲线上G-扭的局部平凡性从特殊纤维（剩余域上）提升到整体曲线（Hensel环上），并建立局部-整体原理，推广Gille-Parimala-Suresh的工作，以及获得Drinfeld-Simpson定理的Hensel版本和Brauer-Azumaya群的新单射性结果。

Method: 采用几何方法，利用扭子的紧化以及梳子磨光技巧（comb smoothing technique）的相对和算术版本，在Kollár和Graber-Harris-Starr工作的基础上详细发展该技术。

Result: (1) 若中心同源核是étale的或剩余域κ足够大，则E_κ→C_κ的Zariski局部平凡性蕴含E→C的Zariski局部平凡性；(2) 在仅假设rad(G)是等平凡的情况下，证明了该结果的平均形式；(3) 对射影齐性空间，在无限制条件下得到类似结论；(4) 得到扭子的局部-整体原理、Drinfeld-Simpson定理的Hensel版本及Brauer-Azumaya群的单射性。

Conclusion: 本文通过几何方法（紧化与梳子磨光技巧）成功将纤维上的局部平凡性提升到整体曲线上，建立了重要的局部-整体原理，并获得了若干重要推论，推进了对Hensel环上曲线G-扭及Brauer群的理解。

Abstract: Let $R$ be a Henselian local ring, let $κ$ be the residue field of $R$, let $C$ be a smooth projective curve over $R$ with geometrically connected fibers, let $G$ be a reductive $C$-group with isotrivial radical torus $\mathrm{rad}(G)$, and let $E\to C$ be a $G$-torsor. We show that, if either the kernel of the central isogeny $G^{\mathrm{sc}}\times_C \mathrm{rad}(G)\to G$ is étale over $C$ or $κ$ is large, the Zariski-local triviality of $E_κ\to C_κ$ implies the Zariski-local triviality of $E\to C$. We also prove an averaged form of this result, assuming only that $\mathrm{rad}(G)$ is isotrivial, as well as a variant for projective homogeneous spaces under no restrictions on $G$. As consequences, we obtain a local-global principle for torsors over function fields of curves over Henselian discrete valuation rings, strengthening work of Gille--Parimala--Suresh, a Henselian version of a theorem of Drinfeld--Simpson, and an injectivity result for the Brauer--Azumaya group of $C$ not covered by earlier work of Colliot-Thélène--Ojanguren--Parimala. Our proofs are geometric and rely on compactifications of torsors and on a relative and arithmetic version of the comb smoothing technique, which we develop in detail, building on work of Kollár and Graber--Harris--Starr.

</details>


### [121] [On generic finiteness of pluricanonical maps of threefolds of general type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02066)
*Tianyue Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了：对于所有满足 $P_2(X)\geq 2$ 的一般型极小三维代数簇 $X$，线性系 $|6K_X|$ 定义了一个一般有限映射，且该结果是最优的。此外，还证明了对于所有一般型极小三维代数簇 $X$，当 $n\geq 38$ 时 $|nK_X|$ 定义一般有限映射。关键技术包括一个新的曲面一般有限性判别准则和一个特殊消解下的有效比较不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定一般型极小三维簇上典范映射（pluricanonical map）何时为一般有限映射，这是双有理几何中的核心问题之一。已有结果中最优的常数为 $6$（在附加条件 $P_2\geq 2$ 下），且该常数不可改进。

Method: 引入了两个关键技术：1) 一个针对曲面的新的一般有限性判别准则；2) 在特殊消解下建立的有效比较不等式。这些工具被用于分析三维簇的 pluricanonical 映射。

Result: 1) 当 $P_2(X)\geq 2$ 时，$|6K_X|$ 给出一般有限映射，且 $6$ 是最优常数；2) 无附加条件时，$n\geq 38$ 保证 $|nK_X|$ 为一般有限映射。

Conclusion: 该结果推进了一般型三维簇 pluricanonical 映射有限性的研究，确定了在 $P_2\geq 2$ 条件下的最优界 $6$，并给出了无条件的一般界 $38$。

Abstract: We prove that $|6K_X|$ defines a generically finite map for all minimal 3-folds $X$ of general type with $P_2(X)\geq 2$, which is optimal. We also prove that $|nK_X|$ defines a generically finite map for all minimal 3-folds $X$ of general type when $n\geq 38$. The essential technical ingredients of this paper are a new generic finiteness criterion for surfaces and an effective comparison inequality under a special resolution.

</details>


### [122] [Positive Cones of Parabolic Grassmann Bundle over a curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02080)
*Ashima Bansal,Shivam Vats*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文定义了光滑射影簇上抛物向量丛的抛物Grassmann丛，推广了抛物射影丛的构造，确定了其Néron-Severi群，并计算了在光滑射影曲线上该丛的nef锥、伪有效锥和Mori锥，以及两个抛物Grassmann丛纤维积的对应锥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将抛物射影丛的构造推广到更一般的抛物Grassmann丛情形，并研究其几何性质（如各种锥的描述）。

Method: 通过定义抛物Grassmann丛推广已有构造，并利用代数几何方法确定其Néron-Severi群及计算各种锥。

Result: 确定了抛物Grassmann丛的Néron-Severi群；在光滑射影曲线上计算了其nef锥、伪有效锥和Mori锥；也计算了两个抛物Grassmann丛纤维积的相应锥。

Conclusion: 成功推广了抛物射影丛构造，并系统描述了抛物Grassmann丛在曲线上及其纤维积的双有理几何不变量。

Abstract: In this article, we define the parabolic Grassmann bundle associated to a parabolic vector bundle over a smooth projective variety, generalizing the construction of parabolic projective bundles developed in \cite{BL}. We determine its Néron--Severi group and compute its nef, pseudoeffective, and Mori cones over smooth projective curves. We also compute the corresponding cones for the fiber product of two parabolic Grassmann bundles over a smooth projective curve.

</details>


### [123] [Recent progress on the geometric Bombieri--Lang conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02165)
*Junyi Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文综述了特征零函数域上几何Bombieri-Lang猜想的最新进展，重点讨论了Xie-Yuan和Guoquan Gao的工作，这些工作共同证明了该猜想对于允许有限态射到阿贝尔簇的簇成立。其核心思想是与Xinyi Yuan合作发展的：在该设定下，Vojta字典可以被具体化——从大高度的有理点可以构造复纤维上的整曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何Bombieri-Lang猜想是算术几何中的重要问题，旨在理解函数域上代数簇的有理点分布。该猜想将有理点的高度与几何结构联系起来，其证明对于深化对数论与几何之间关系的理解具有重要意义。

Method: 作者通过综述Xie-Yuan和Guoquan Gao的最新工作，展示了如何利用Vojta字典将大高度的有理点转化为复纤维上的整曲线，从而证明对于能有限态射到阿贝尔簇的簇，几何Bombieri-Lang猜想成立。

Result: Xie-Yuan和Guoquan Gao的工作共同证明了特征零函数域上几何Bombieri-Lang猜想对于允许有限态射到阿贝尔簇的簇成立。

Conclusion: 该进展表明Vojta字典在函数域设定下可以具体实现，为大高度有理点构造复纤维上的整曲线提供了有效方法，为最终解决一般情形的几何Bombieri-Lang猜想奠定了基础。

Abstract: We survey recent progress on the geometric Bombieri--Lang conjecture over function fields of characteristic zero. We discuss recent work of Xie--Yuan and Guoquan Gao, which together proves the conjecture for varieties admitting finite morphisms to abelian varieties. The guiding idea, developed in joint work with Xinyi Yuan, is that Vojta's dictionary can be made concrete in this setting: from rational points of large height one constructs entire curves on complex fibers.

</details>


### [124] [Quasi-$F$-splitting versus log canonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02218)
*Kenta Sato,Shunsuke Takagi,Shou Yoshikawa*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究拟F-分裂与对数典范性之间的关系，证明若数值Q-Gorenstein正规奇点在每个e≥1下都是拟F^e-分裂的，则它是数值对数典范的；在二维情况下，当Gorenstein指标不被特征p整除时，证明了逆命题，并对二维拟F-分裂正规奇点进行了分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拟F-分裂（quasi-F-splitting）与对数典范性（log canonicity）这两个奇点理论中的重要概念之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用数值Q-Gorenstein正规奇点的理论框架，结合特征p代数几何方法，在二维情况下考虑Gorenstein指标与特征p的可除性条件。

Result: (1) 若数值Q-Gorenstein正规奇点在每个e≥1下都是拟F^e-分裂的，则它是数值对数典范的；(2) 在二维且Gorenstein指标不被特征p整除的条件下，逆命题成立；(3) 完成了二维拟F-分裂正规奇点的分类。

Conclusion: 拟F-分裂性与对数典范性在特定条件下具有等价关系，且二维拟F-分裂正规奇点可被完全分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the relationship between quasi-$F$-splitting and log canonicity. We show that if a numerically $\mathbb{Q}$-Gorenstein normal singularity is quasi-$F^e$-split for every $e\geq 1$, then it is numerically log canonical. In dimension two, we prove the converse under the condition that the Gorenstein index is not divisible by the characteristic $p$. We also classify two-dimensional quasi-$F$-split normal singularities.

</details>


### [125] [Components of simple and non--simple type of Hurwitz schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02240)
*Ciro Ciliberto,Andreas Leopold Knutsen,Sara Torelli*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hurwitz空间$\mathcal{H}_{g \to b,d; \mathbf{e}}$中非简单型（即所有成员都通过中间曲线因子分解）的分量存在的充要条件，并证明了对$b\geq 2$总是存在简单型分量，而对$b\in\{0,1\}$则在适当充分条件下存在，但存在反例表明简单型分量可能不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Hurwitz空间中何时存在所有成员都通过中间曲线因子分解的分量（非简单型），并刻画简单型分量的存在性条件。

Method: 通过分析Hurwitz空间的分量结构，给出非简单型分量存在的充要条件；并针对基曲线亏格$b$的不同取值（$b\geq 2$和$b\in\{0,1\}$）分别讨论简单型分量的存在性。

Result: 给出了非简单型分量存在的充要条件；对于$b\geq 2$，总是存在简单型分量；对于$b\in\{0,1\}$，在一定充分条件下存在简单型分量，但也存在该条件下简单型分量不存在的反例。

Conclusion: Hurwitz空间中分量的"简单性"与基曲线亏格密切相关：高亏格$(b\geq 2)$保证简单型分量存在，而低亏格$(b=0,1)$则情况更为复杂，需要额外条件。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal{H}_{g \to b,d; \mathbf{e}}$, with $\mathbf{e}=(e_1,\ldots, e_n)$, be the Hurwitz space, parametrizing all morphisms $π: C\to B$ of degree $d$, with $n$ points $x_1,\ldots, x_n\in C$ of ramification order $e_1,\ldots, e_n$ respectively, and where $C$ and $B$ are smooth, irreducible, projective curves of genera $g$ and $b$ respectively. In this paper we study the question of when there exist components of $\mathcal{H}_{g \to b,d; \mathbf{e}}$ whose members $π: C \to B$ all factor through an intermediate curve, in which case we say that these components are \emph{of non--simple type}. We give necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of components of non--simple type. Then we prove that for $b\geq 2$ there are always components of simple type, and for $b\in \{0,1\}$ there are such components under suitable sufficient conditions. However there are easy examples for $b\in \{0,1\}$ in which there are never components of simple type.

</details>


### [126] [The Gorenstein property and Pixton's conjecture for compact type moduli](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02249)
*Samir Canning,Hannah Larson,Johannes Schmitt*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了当$g\geq 2$且$2g+n\geq 12$时，$\mathcal{M}_{g,n}^{\mathrm{ct}}$的tautological环不是Gorenstein的；并证明了Pixton猜想的若干新情形，包括$\mathcal{M}_{6}^{\mathrm{ct}}$、$\mathcal{M}_{5,2}^{\mathrm{ct}}$和$\mathcal{M}_7^{\mathrm{ct}}$，这是首次在tautological环非Gorenstein的情况下验证Pixton猜想成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究模空间$\mathcal{M}_{g,n}^{\mathrm{ct}}$上tautological环的代数结构性质（Gorenstein性质），以及验证Pixton关于3-spin关系完全描述tautological环的猜想，这些结果对于理解主极化阿贝尔簇模空间上的非tautological环具有关键作用。

Method: 通过代数几何与模空间理论的方法，分析tautological环的Gorenstein性质；利用3-spin关系验证Pixton猜想在特定模空间上的成立性。

Result: 1) 对于$g\geq 2$且$2g+n\geq 12$，$\mathcal{M}_{g,n}^{\mathrm{ct}}$的tautological环非Gorenstein；2) Pixton猜想在$\mathcal{M}_{6}^{\mathrm{ct}}$、$\mathcal{M}_{5,2}^{\mathrm{ct}}$和$\mathcal{M}_7^{\mathrm{ct}}$上成立，这是首次在非Gorenstein情形下验证该猜想。

Conclusion: Pixton猜想可在tautological环非Gorenstein的条件下成立，这一结果为后续研究主极化阿贝尔簇模空间上的非tautological环提供了关键工具。

Abstract: We show that the tautological ring of $\mathcal{M}_{g,n}^{\mathrm{ct}}$ is not Gorenstein for $g\geq 2$ and $2g+n\geq 12$. We prove new cases of Pixton's conjecture that the $3$-spin relations are a complete set of relations for the tautological ring, including $\mathcal{M}_{6}^{\mathrm{ct}}$, $\mathcal{M}_{5,2}^{\mathrm{ct}}$, and $\mathcal{M}_7^{\mathrm{ct}}$. These are the first known cases where Pixton's conjecture is true, but the tautological ring is not Gorenstein. These results are also a key ingredient in recent work on non-tautological cycles on the moduli space of principally polarized abelian varieties.

</details>


### [127] [Bridgeland-Enriques general K3 surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02281)
*Ziqi Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Bridgeland-Enriques一般K3曲面的概念，源于对K3曲面上Enriques范畴与Bridgeland稳定性条件不变量的研究。次数10的Bridgeland-Enriques一般K3曲面族检测到特殊Gushel-Mukai三维簇的范畴退化；更高次数的族与Hodge特殊Gushel-Mukai四维簇及双EPW六次曲面密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受K3曲面上Enriques范畴以及Bridgeland稳定性条件不变量的研究启发，旨在引入并系统地定义Bridgeland-Enriques一般K3曲面这一新概念，以揭示其在代数几何中与Gushel-Mukai簇及双EPW六次曲面的深层联系。

Method: 通过结合Enriques范畴理论与Bridgeland稳定性条件的不变量，定义了Bridgeland-Enriques一般K3曲面族；利用次数10的族检测特殊Gushel-Mukai三维簇的范畴退化；通过更高次数族与Hodge特殊Gushel-Mukai四维簇及双EPW六次曲面的关联进行结构分析。

Result: 成功定义了Bridgeland-Enriques一般K3曲面；次数10的族揭示了特殊Gushel-Mukai三维簇的范畴退化现象；更高次数的族与Hodge特殊Gushel-Mukai四维簇及双EPW六次曲面建立了密切联系。

Conclusion: Bridgeland-Enriques一般K3曲面为理解Gushel-Mukai簇的范畴结构以及Hodge理论中的特殊现象提供了新的视角和工具，并为进一步研究Enriques范畴与稳定性条件在代数几何中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This article introduces a notion of Bridgeland-Enriques general K3 surfaces motivated by the study of Enriques categories over K3 surfaces and the invariant Bridgeland stability conditions. The family of Bridgeland-Enriques general K3 surfaces of degree 10 detects a categorical degeneration of special Gushel-Mukai threefolds. Also, the families of Bridgeland-Enriques general K3 surfaces with higher degrees are closely related to Hodge-special Gushel-Mukai fourfolds and double EPW sextics.

</details>

### [128] [A homotopy coherent Pontryagin-Thom isomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01482)
*Kenneth Blakey,Liam Keenan*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文构建了带转移的光滑流形上同伦不变层构成的对称幺半稳定∞-范畴，其单位对象恰好是（有结构的）几何配边，并证明了单位层的自同态环可典范地等同于相应的𝔼_∞-Thom环谱，从而给出了庞特里亚金-汤姆同构的𝔼_∞-提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的庞特里亚金-汤姆同构断言（有结构的）几何配边对应的乘法上同调论与关联Thom谱确定的上同调论同构。作者希望将该同构提升到𝔼_∞-环谱层面，即在更高阶的代数结构中建立这种对应关系。

Method: 构造了一个带转移的光滑流形上同伦不变层构成的 presentably 对称幺半稳定∞-范畴，使得其单位对象恰为几何配边；然后通过分析单位层的自同态环，证明其与相应的𝔼_∞-Thom环谱典范同构。

Result: 成功构建了所需的∞-范畴，并证明了单位层的自同态环与𝔼_∞-Thom环谱之间的典范同构，即实现了庞特里亚金-汤姆同构的𝔼_∞-提升。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的庞特里亚金-汤姆同构从谱群层面提升到了𝔼_∞-环谱层面，为几何配边与Thom谱之间的代数结构对应提供了更高阶的范畴论框架。

Abstract: Classically, the Pontryagin-Thom isomorphism asserts that the multiplicative cohomology theory given by (structured) geometric cobordism is isomorphic to the cohomology theory determined by an associated Thom spectrum. We construct a presentably symmetric monoidal stable $\infty$-category of homotopy invariant sheaves with transfers on smooth manifolds whose unit is precisely (structured) geometric cobordism. We show the endomorphism ring of the unit sheaf can be canonically identified with the associated $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-Thom ring spectrum, i.e., we provide an $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-lift of the Pontryagin-Thom isomorphism.

</details>


### [129] [Reduced characteristic number criteria for equivariant bordism of $T^k$- and $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^k$-manifolds with isolated fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01889)
*Runze Chen,Zhi Lü,Leqi Yang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文针对两类具有孤立不动点的等变流形（酉T^k-流形和光滑(ℤ₂)^k-流形），建立了简化的等变配边判别准则，分别仅依赖于等变陈类的单个多项式和最高等变Stiefel-Whitney类的幂次，并以此导出关于Kosniowski猜想的关键不等式，部分验证了该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典等变配边理论需要计算全部等变特征数来判定等变流形是否等变配边于零，计算量巨大。本文旨在为具有孤立不动点的两类等变流形建立简化的配边判别准则，降低计算复杂度，并以此探索Kosniowski猜想中关于欧拉示性数线性下界的存在性问题。

Method: 对于酉T^k-流形，利用单个等变陈类多项式构建配边判别准则；引入最小区分度（minimal distinguishing degree）概念，导出关于流形维数dim M与欧拉示性数χ(M)的两个关键不等式。对于光滑(ℤ₂)^k-流形，仅依赖最高等变Stiefel-Whitney类的幂次给出更简洁的配边准则。

Result: 建立了酉T^k-流形的简化等变配边准则；获得了最小区分度的两个不等式，刻画了dim M与χ(M)的关系；解决了Kosniowski猜想中χ(M)线性下界的存在性问题，并在自然可允许假设下部分验证了该猜想；当dim M=2k时，给出了Kosniowski猜想环面推广的另一种证明。对(ℤ₂)^k-流形，得到了更简洁的等变配边准则。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入最小区分度等新概念，大幅降低了等变配边判别的计算需求，并为Kosniowski猜想提供了新的理论支撑和部分验证。方法具有普适性，可推广至更一般的情形。

Abstract: Classical equivariant bordism theories require computing the full collection of equivariant characteristic numbers to detect whether an equivariant manifold bounds equivariantly or not. This paper establishes simplified equivariant bordism characterizations for two families of equivariant manifolds with isolated fixed points: unitary $T^k$-manifolds and closed smooth $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^k$-manifolds. For any unitary $T^k$-manifold $M$ with isolated fixed points, we establish an equivariant unitary bordism criterion built entirely from a single polynomial of equivariant Chern classes. We further introduce the minimal distinguishing degree and obtain two key inequalities that capture the interplay between $\dim M$ and the Euler characteristic $χ(M)$ through this minimal distinguishing degree. These inequalities settle the existence problem of a linear lower bound for $χ(M)$ within the framework of Kosniowski's conjecture and partially verify the conjecture under natural admissible assumptions. We also provide an alternative proof settling the toric generalization of Kosniowski's conjecture when $\dim M=2k$. By contrast, for a closed smooth $(\mathbb{Z}_2)^k$-manifold with isolated fixed points, we derive a more concise equivariant bordism criterion relying solely on the powers of the top equivariant Stiefel-Whitney class. Our new criteria substantially reduce computational demands.

</details>


### [130] [Equivariant twisted $R$-algebras via Thom spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02060)
*Samik Basu,Abhinandan Das*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究 $C_2$-交换环谱 $R$ 上的扭曲 $R$-代数结构，构造了偶数实交换环谱的各种商作为 Thom 谱，并计算了 $K\mathbb{R}/2$ 的实拓扑 Hochschild 同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $C_2$-等变同调论中，研究带有 $C_2$ 作用交换环谱的代数结构，特别是构造和理解扭曲代数结构及其商对象。

Method: 通过 $S^1$ 和 $U(n)$ 上的 $C_2$ 作用构造 Thom 谱，进而实现各种商结构；利用 $gl_1K\mathbb{R}$ 的单位谱分裂进行计算。

Result: 给出了 $K\mathbb{R}$ 的扭曲 $K\mathbb{R}$-代数结构；构造了实配边谱 $M\mathbb{R}$ 的商、实 2-周期 Morava K-理论作为实 Morava E-理论的模；计算了 $K\mathbb{R}/2$ 的实拓扑 Hochschild 同调。

Conclusion: 扭曲 $R$-代数框架为等变同调论中的代数结构提供了统一描述，且 Thom 谱方法能够有效计算实拓扑 Hochschild 同调。

Abstract: For a $C_2$-commutative ring spectrum $R$, a twisted $R$-algebra is an $R$-module with a multiplication whose order is switched by the $C_2$-action. In this paper, we construct various quotients of $R$ as twisted $R$-algebras, when $R$ is an even real commutative ring spectrum. These are constructed as Thom spectra of maps out of suitable $C_2$-actions on $S^1$ and $U(n)$. One such example is given by $K\mathbb{R}$ which is endowed with a twisted $K\mathbb{R}$-algebra structure. Other examples include quotients such as $M\mathbb{R}/(2,x_1,\dots, x_{n-1})$ over the real bordism spectrum $M\mathbb{R}$, and the real $2$-periodic Morava $K$-theories as modules over the real Morava $E$-theory spectra. In the context of twisted $R$-algebras, one may consider the real topological Hochschild homology, and for Thom spectra, one has a nice formula again as a Thom spectrum. We use this to obtain computations for the real topological Hochschild homology of $K\mathbb{R}/2$ as a twisted $K\mathbb{R}$-algebra. The computation also involves a splitting of the units spectrum $gl_1K\mathbb{R}$, which is an analogue of the classical splitting of the units of $K$-theory.

</details>

### [131] [Unitriangular R-matrices of quantum affine algebras and Yangians via Theta series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02287)
*Huafeng Zhang*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文给出了量子仿射代数中单位三角R-矩阵在任意有限维表示下的简单共轭公式，涉及Frenkel-Hernandez的T-级数和之前引入的Theta级数，并推广到了Yangian情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子仿射代数的通用R-矩阵具有Gauss分解形式，但其中单位三角部分的显式表达式在任意有限维表示评估下尚不明确，需要更简洁的共轭公式来刻画其结构。

Method: 利用Frenkel-Hernandez的T-级数与之前引入的Theta级数，构造单位三角R-矩阵的共轭公式；在Yangian情形中，借助移位Yangian三重张量积表示的结合子（associators）进行推广。

Result: 给出了量子仿射代数单位三角R-矩阵在任意有限维表示下的简单共轭公式，并将该公式成功推广到了Yangian情形。

Conclusion: 本文为量子仿射代数及Yangian的单位三角R-矩阵提供了统一的共轭描述，丰富了量子群与Yangian的表示论工具。

Abstract: The universal R-matrix of the quantum affine algebra associated to a finite-dimensional simple complex Lie algebra admits a Gauss decomposition into an uper unitriangular part, an abelian part, and a lower unitriangular part. In this paper, we provide a simple conjugation formula for the unitriangular R-matrices with one tensor factor evaluated at an arbitrary finite-dimensional representation of the quantum affine algebra. Our formula involves the T-series of Frenkel--Hernandez and the Theta series introduced in a previous work.
  We also extend our conjugation formula to the Yangian case, making use of associators for triple tensor product representations of shifted Yangians.

</details>

### [132] [Inflation from Covariant Signature Change: A Geometric Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01274)
*Raghvendra Singh,Sergey Bondarenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协变机制，通过度规从欧几里得到洛伦兹号的平滑转变驱动有限时段的加速膨胀，在曲率正则的退化超曲面上实现，无需引入暴胀子场，并与无边界边界条件兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一种从欧几里得几何起源到洛伦兹暴涨阶段的无奇点、无暴胀子的协变路径，避免传统暴涨模型中初始奇点问题。

Method: 通过标量内插函数沿类时汇实现度规号的连续转变；在退化超曲面处用协变延拓构造有效源，将爱因斯坦张量重写为局域化的几何应力张量；推导依赖于内插函数斜率的局域加速度判据。

Result: 导出了模型无关的局域加速度判据：当内插函数斜率超过由外在曲率和空间 Ricci 曲率决定的临界值时暴胀持续，首次达到饱和时结束；针对 tanh、广义逻辑函数及幂律/反正切等典型光滑剖面得到了加速时段的闭式解。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了一种从正则欧几里得起源到早期洛伦兹暴涨相的非奇异、无暴胀子途径，剖面形状控制加速时长，与无边界边界条件相容。

Abstract: We present a covariant mechanism in which a smooth change of metric signature, from a Euclidean to a Lorentzian regime, drives a finite interval of accelerated expansion. The transition, encoded by a scalar interpolator along a timelike congruence, occurs on a codimension-one hypersurface where the continued metric is degenerate but curvature invariants remain finite, so the surface is curvature-regular. Using this covariant continuation, we rewrite the Einstein tensor of the continued metric as a localized, interpolator-dependent effective source for the post-transition Lorentzian branch, yielding a purely geometric stress tensor supported near the crossing. In the Lorentzian regime, we derive a model-independent, local criterion for acceleration: inflation persists while the interpolator's slope exceeds a critical value fixed by the extrinsic curvature and the spatial Ricci curvature on the initial hypersurface, and ends when this inequality is first saturated. Standard smooth profiles (tanh, generalized logistic, and power-law/arctan) admit closed-form expressions for the proper-time duration of the accelerated epoch, showing that, for fixed geometric data, the profile shape controls this duration. The construction provides a non-singular, inflaton-free route from a regular Euclidean origin to an early Lorentzian phase of accelerated expansion, in a manner compatible with no--boundary--type boundary conditions.

</details>


### [133] [Hypersurface Anchored Variational Principle for General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01285)
*Prasanta Sahoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种嵌入超曲面的广义相对论变分推广，通过在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量中增加一个依赖于嵌入超曲面的微分同胚不变泛函，并独立变化超曲面的嵌入，得到带有局域分布贡献的爱因斯坦方程和确定允许嵌入的锚定方程。该系统不引入额外传播自由度，锚定条件不同于规范选择，在正则形式下狄拉克代数在体区域恢复，仅在超曲面处有分布性表面贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义相对论中引入一种新的变分约束机制，通过超曲面的独立变分来定义经典解空间的约束子集，从而构造一类特殊的时空几何结构。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量中补充一个依赖于嵌入类空超曲面的微分同胚不变泛函，对时空度量和超曲面嵌入独立变分，导出体区域的爱因斯坦方程（含分布项）和超曲面的锚定方程。利用隐函数定理和椭圆估计分析锚定超曲面的局部存在性与线性稳定性，并在正则形式下检验约束代数的闭合性。

Result: 得到了带有分布贡献的爱因斯坦场方程和锚定方程；锚定条件在椭圆性和可逆性假设下具备局部持久性和线性稳定性；锚定条件与局域切片规范条件不等价；正则形式下动量约束保持标准形式，哈密顿约束获得超曲面支撑项；约束代数在体区域恢复狄拉克代数，偏差仅限局域分布性表面项；在均匀宇宙学中锚定表面诱导匹配条件，允许膨胀率的有限跃迁。

Conclusion: 该构造定义了广义相对论经典解空间的一个约束子集，由至少允许一个满足锚定方程的嵌入超曲面的时空构成。锚定条件是一种真正的变分约束而非坐标选择，不引入额外传播自由度，并在正则层面保持了约束代数的弱闭合性。

Abstract: A hypersurface anchored variational extension of General Relativity is formulated in which the Einstein-Hilbert action is supplemented by a diffeomorphism invariant functional supported on an embedded spacelike hypersurface whose embedding is varied independently of the spacetime metric. The resulting Euler-Lagrange system consists of the Einstein equations with a localized distributional contribution together with an anchoring equation determining admissible embeddings. For the admissible class of hypersurface functionals considered here, the bulk field equations retain the standard second order principal structure away from the hypersurface and no additional propagating bulk gravitational degrees of freedom are introduced. Under ellipticity and invertibility assumptions, local persistence and linear stability of anchoring hypersurfaces follow from standard implicit function and elliptic estimates. The anchoring condition is generically inequivalent to local slicing gauge conditions and therefore defines a genuine variational restriction rather than a coordinate choice. In the canonical formulation, the momentum constraints retain their standard form, whereas the Hamiltonian constraint acquires a hypersurface supported term. The corresponding smeared generators close in the weak sense: the Dirac algebra is recovered in the bulk, with deviations confined to localized distributional surface contributions. The construction therefore defines a constrained sector of the classical solution space of General Relativity consisting of spacetimes that admit at least one embedded hypersurface satisfying the anchoring equation. In homogeneous cosmology, the localized term induces matching conditions across the anchoring surface, allowing finite transitions in the expansion rate while preserving standard evolution away from the transition hypersurface.

</details>


### [134] [Impact of Spacecraft Orbit Uncertainties and Velocity Mismodeling on the LISA Gravitational-Wave Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01318)
*Lorenzo Speri,Olaf Hartwig,Waldemar Martens,Oliver Jennrich,Eric Joffre,Michele Armano,Martin Hewitson,Nora Lützgendorf*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LISA航天器轨道不确定性（位置/速度误差）如何通过引力波响应传播至探测器输出和参数估计，发现轨道误差在高频段影响响应知识（最差失配<10⁻⁷），而忽略航天器速度在10⁻⁴ Hz附近产生最大影响（失配~10⁻⁴），对典型银河系双星系统（频率10⁻⁴ Hz，SNR=200，观测1年）参数估计偏差小于1σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA数据研究中通常假设航天器轨道完全已知，但实际轨道确定过程会引入位置和速度不确定性；需要量化这些不确定性如何传播到探测器输出，并评估其对引力波数据分析的影响。

Method: 通过误差传播分析，量化航天器轨道不确定性对LISA响应知识的影响；使用失配（mismatch）指标评估响应误差大小；分析忽略航天器速度对参数估计的偏差影响，以银河系双星系统为案例。

Result: 轨道不确定性在高频段影响响应知识，最差失配低于10⁻⁷；忽略航天器速度在10⁻⁴ Hz附近失配达~10⁻⁴量级；对频率10⁻⁴ Hz、SNR=200、观测1年的银河双星，忽略速度导致参数估计偏差小于1σ。

Conclusion: 这是首次从引力波应变经探测器输出到参数估计的完整误差传播表征，表明当前航天器轨道精度足以满足LISA科学目标，但为未来更高精度需求提供了基准。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational wave observatory that consists of three spacecraft in a near-equilateral triangular formation. The spacecraft orbits are typically assumed to be perfectly known in LISA data analysis studies, but in reality, the orbit determination process introduces uncertainties in the spacecraft positions and velocities. In this work, we investigate how these uncertainties propagate into the LISA detector output and the impact of neglecting the spacecraft velocities. We quantify these errors in the knowledge of the LISA response using mismatches and discuss the implications for gravitational wave data analysis. We find that spacecraft orbit uncertainties impact the LISA response knowledge at high frequencies with worst mismatch below $10^{-7}$. The effect of neglecting the spacecraft velocities is largest at frequencies around $10^{-4}$ Hz with mismatches of order $10^{-4}$. For a galactic binary with frequency $10^{-4}$ Hz and SNR=200 observed for one year, we find that neglecting the spacecraft velocities in the response leads to less than 1-$σ$ biases in the parameter estimates. This work provides the first characterization of how errors in the LISA gravitational wave response propagate from gravitational wave strain through detector output to estimated parameters.

</details>


### [135] [Vacuum Cherenkov radiation in supercritical magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01419)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极端强电磁场条件下真空的非线性效应，特别是光速变化引发的切连科夫辐射现象，以检验欧拉-海森堡理论的有效性。

Method: 基于欧拉-海森堡有效场理论，计算临界和超临界磁场强度下真空折射率的变化，进而分析带电粒子产生的切连科夫辐射特性，并与同步辐射进行对比。

Result: 给出了不同磁场强度下切连科夫辐射的特征，以及与同步辐射的差异对比。

Conclusion: 极强磁场下真空的量子电动力学效应可导致切连科夫辐射，该辐射与同步辐射具有不同的特征，可作为检验非线性真空理论的探针。

Abstract: In the presence of very intense electromagnetic fields, the refractive index of vacuum is modified such that light velocity is less than $c$ and ultrarelativistic charged particles can be faster than light and can induce Cherenkov radiation. We present the comparison of the Cherenkov radiation produced by the Euler-Heisenberg theory for critical and supercritical magnetic fields. We also make the comparison between the Cherenkov and the synchrotron radiation produced by the charged particles.

</details>


### [136] [Stability regions of glued wormholes with massless Kim-Lee backreacted spacetimes as interior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01687)
*G. F. Akhtaryanova,R. Kh. Karimov,R. N. Izmailov,A. Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有零ADM质量内部的粘合虫洞的稳定性，通过薄壳动力学分析了两种不同内部时空（标量荷和电荷Kim-Lee虫洞）与外部Schwarzschild/Reissner-Nordström时空粘合后的稳定区域，发现两种情况下稳定区域几乎相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零ADM质量虫洞（如Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞）在天体物理微引力透镜中有重要应用，但单独存在时对小扰动不稳定。本文探索将此类虫洞作为内部伙伴，与外部有质量时空通过薄壳粘合后能否实现稳定。

Method: 采用Garcia、Lobo和Visser提出的薄壳"质量"和"外力"约束概念，通过薄壳动力学分析粘合虫洞的稳定区域。考虑两类情况：零ADM质量内部为Kim-Lee虫洞，分别粘合到外部Schwarzschild真空和Reissner-Nordström时空。

Result: 两类情况下（标量荷与电荷的Kim-Lee内部时空），粘合虫洞的稳定区域几乎相似，尽管两种内部时空物理性质截然不同。详细分析了实现粘合虫洞稳定的条件。

Conclusion: 零ADM质量内部虫洞可以通过与外部有质量时空粘合实现稳定，且稳定区域对内部时空的具体物理性质（标量荷或电荷）不敏感。

Abstract: Asymptotic zero Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) mass wormholes, such as the zero-mass traversable Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole, are of great interest for astrophysical applications such as in the galactic microlensing. However, when considered individually, they are unstable to small perturbations. On the other hand, there is a possibility that they can be stable as an interior partner of a traversable glued wormhole obtained by suitably gluing the interior to the observationally relevant massive exterior spacetimes across spherically symmetric thin shells. Although the exterior spacetime has non-zero ADM mass, massless interior partner remains massless sharing the stability of the glued wormhole. The dynamics of the thin-shell then demarcates the stability regions of the glued wormhole that we wish to study here by employing the novel concepts of thin-shell "mass" and of "external force" constraints discovered by Garcia, Lobo and Visser. We shall consider two classes, where the zero ADM mass interior are Kim-Lee wormholes glued to the exterior Schwarzschild vacuum and Reissner-Nordström spacetime respectively. It turns out that the stability regions in both cases are almost similar although the two interior Kim-Lee spacetimes are physically very different, one scalar charged and the other electrically charged. The conditions under which the stability of glued wormholes could be achieved are analyzed in detail.

</details>


### [137] [Particle dynamics and quasi-periodic oscillations of a Reissner--Nordström-like black hole in Kalb--Ramond gravity under an external magnetic test field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01723)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,Sardor Murodov,Bekzod Rahmatov,Javlon Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond引力中带电Reissner-Nordström-like黑洞周围带电测试粒子的动力学与准周期振荡（QPO），在外部磁测试场存在下，推导了运动方程与特征频率，并通过MCMC分析用GRO J1655-40、XTE J1550-564和M82 X-1的观测数据约束了模型参数，结果表明该模型能一致再现观测QPO对，可作为探测洛伦兹破缺黑洞几何的唯象工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在Kalb-Ramond引力背景（含洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ）下，带电黑洞周围带电粒子的动力学行为及QPO特征，并利用实际天文观测数据约束该修正引力模型参数，检验其作为探测强引力环境中洛伦兹破缺效应的可行性。

Method: 在带电KR黑洞背景上，从无源Maxwell方程构造磁测试场（非标准Wald型）；推导带电测试粒子的运动方程、有效势、圆轨道条件及轨道与径向epicyclic频率；将所得频率应用于相对论进动模型（上QPO为轨道频率，下QPO为近星点进动频率）；用GRO J1655-40、XTE J1550-564、M82 X-1的孪生峰QPO数据进行MCMC分析，约束黑洞电荷Q/M、KR参数ℓ、粒子比电荷ε及磁耦合β=bM。

Result: 黑洞电荷Q/M、KR参数ℓ、比电荷ε和磁耦合β共同影响ISCO和QPO频率；MCMC后验约束表明带电KR黑洞模型在所选参数范围内能一致再现观测QPO对；QPO观测可作为探测洛伦兹破缺黑洞几何和强引力电磁效应的有效唯象工具。

Conclusion: QPO观测有望成为约束修改引力理论参数的有力手段，特别适用于探测洛伦兹破缺效应与电磁场在强引力环境中的联合效应。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles and quasi-periodic oscillations around a Reissner--Nordström-like black hole in Kalb--Ramond (KR) gravity in the presence of an external magnetic test field. The KR background introduces a Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell$, which modifies the spacetime geometry, horizon structure, circular orbits, and characteristic frequencies of particle motion. In contrast to the standard Wald-type prescription, the magnetic-field configuration is constructed from the source-free Maxwell equation on the charged KR background, allowing the magnetic profile to be consistently adapted to the modified geometry. We derive the equations of motion, the effective potential, the conditions for circular orbits, and the orbital and radial epicyclic frequencies of charged particles. The results show that the black-hole charge $Q/M$, the KR parameter $\ell$, the specific particle charge $ε$, and the magnetic coupling $β=bM$ jointly affect the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) frequencies. We then apply the obtained frequencies to the relativistic precession model, where the upper QPO frequency is identified with the orbital frequency and the lower one with the periastron-precession frequency. Using the observed twin-peak QPO data of GRO J1655--40, XTE J1550--564, and M82 X-1, we perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis to constrain the model parameters. The obtained posterior constraints indicate that the charged KR black-hole model with an external magnetic field can consistently reproduce the observed QPO pairs within the adopted parameter ranges. These findings suggest that QPO observations may serve as a useful phenomenological tool for probing Lorentz-violating black-hole geometries and electromagnetic effects in strong-gravity environments.

</details>


### [138] [Classical and Loop Quantum Cosmology of Interacting Dark Energy: A Dynamical System Analysis with Superfluid Dark Matter and Dust Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01724)
*Mohd Shahalam,K. Yerzhanov,G. Bauyrzhan,P. K. Dhankar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在经典爱因斯坦引力与圈量子宇宙学框架下，研究了暗能量与暗物质相互作用的宇宙学动力学。考虑了超流暗物质（广义三次状态方程）与标准无压流体两种暗物质模型，以及广义非线性与常数两种暗能量状态方程。分析了两类唯象相互作用项。经典引力下无压物质模型存在稳定晚期吸引子，而超流暗物质模型仅有鞍点与非双曲临界点。在圈量子宇宙学中，量子几何修正取代了大爆炸奇点，使经典吸引子消失，所有相互作用模型仅剩鞍点与非双曲临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质属性（超流态与无压流体）以及量子引力效应（圈量子宇宙学）如何影响相互作用的暗能量-暗物质系统的渐近演化行为。

Method: 在经典爱因斯坦引力与圈量子宇宙学两种引力框架下，分别构建包含两种暗物质模型（超流暗物质/无压流体）与两种暗能量状态方程（广义非线性/常数）的相互作用系统，引入两种唯象能量转移项Q=αṗ_m和Q=βṗ_d，通过相空间动力学分析（临界点分类与稳定性分析）研究系统演化。

Result: 经典引力下：无压暗物质模型存在稳定晚期吸引子，超流暗物质模型仅有鞍点与非双曲点。圈量子宇宙学中：量子反弹修正改变相空间拓扑，经典稳定吸引子消失，所有相互作用模型仅剩鞍点与非双曲临界点。

Conclusion: 暗物质的状态方程（超流性质）与量子引力修正（圈量子宇宙学）均对相互作用暗区系统的渐近动力学产生显著影响，尤其圈量子宇宙学彻底改变了系统的长期演化结构。

Abstract: We study the cosmological dynamics of interacting dark energy and dark matter in Classical Einstein Gravity and Loop Quantum Cosmology. Two dark matter scenarios are considered: superfluid dark matter described by a generalized cubic equation of state and the standard pressureless fluid. The dark energy component is modeled using both a generalized nonlinear equation of state and a constant equation of state. We examine two phenomenological interaction terms, $Q=α\dotρ_m$ and $Q=β\dotρ_d$, which govern the energy transfer between the dark sectors. In classical gravity, the pressureless matter model exhibits stable late-time attractors, whereas the superfluid dark matter model admits only saddle and non-hyperbolic critical points. Extending the analysis to Loop Quantum Cosmology, quantum geometric corrections replace the Big Bang singularity with a nonsingular quantum bounce, and significantly modify the phase-space dynamics. As a result, the stable attractors of the classical pressureless matter model disappear, and all interacting models possess only saddle and non-hyperbolic critical points. These findings highlight the significant influence of both dark matter properties and quantum gravitational effects on the asymptotic evolution of interacting dark-sectors.

</details>


### [139] [Rotating Black Holes and the Kerr/CFT Correspondence in Einstein-Bumblebee Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01884)
*Yu-Qi Chen,Jin-Yang Shen,Hai-Shan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在五维Einstein-Bumblebee引力中构造了等角动量旋转黑洞（含/不含宇宙常数），通过Wald形式与Komar积分两种方法计算热力学量，发现两者电荷差一个仅由Bumblebee耦合决定的常数因子。Kerr/CFT对偶导出的微观熵精确匹配Komar积分版本的熵而非Wald熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Bumblebee引力（一种洛伦兹对称性自发破缺的理论）中旋转黑洞的热力学性质，并检验不同热力学定义（Wald形式与Komar积分）的等价性，以及全息对偶（Kerr/CFT）的适用性。

Method: 1) 构造五维Einstein-Bumblebee引力中的等角动量旋转黑洞解；2) 分别使用Wald形式（基于Noether荷）和Komar积分（基于守恒流）计算热力学量；3) 应用Kerr/CFT对应计算微观熵。

Result: 1) Wald形式与Komar积分给出的质量、角动量和熵相差一个Bumblebee耦合常数决定的因子；2) Kerr/CFT对偶的微观熵精确匹配Komar积分版本的熵。

Conclusion: 在Bumblebee引力中，Kerr/CFT对偶更自然地与Komar积分定义的熵一致，暗示后者可能是更合适的重力熵定义；Bumblebee耦合破坏了Wald形式与Komar积分的等价性。

Abstract: We constructed rotating black holes with equal angular momentum in five dimensional Einstein-Bumblebee gravity with and without cosmological constant. Their thermodynamic properties are examined via two distinct methods: the Wald formalism and the Komar integral. Notably, the conserved charges, including mass, angular momentum, and entropy, computed from these two approaches differ by a constant prefactor that is solely determined by the Bumblebee coupling. Subsequently, we apply the Kerr/CFT correspondence to derive the microscopic entropy of these black holes and find that it precisely reproduces the entropy in Komar-integral version, rather than the Wald entropy.

</details>


### [140] [Chaotic behaviors of particles around the black hole with an anisotropic matter immersed in a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01910)
*Khusan Alibekov,Hocheol Lee,Yovqochev Pahlavon,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Bum-Hoon Lee,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Wonwoo Lee*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为探索磁化黑洞时空中非可积粒子运动提供新的理论框架，并用于探测银河系中心黑洞——那里磁场可能源于吸积盘或周围等离子体效应。

Method: 采用Harrison变换构造精确解；通过Lyapunov指数分析局部混沌行为；利用庞加莱截面分析全局混沌动力学。

Result: 各向异性物质参数增大抑制局部混沌（Lyapunov指数降低）；外部磁场变化引起全局混沌行为的定性转变，庞加莱截面显示规则与混沌轨迹之间的转换。

Conclusion: 各向异性物质与外部磁场在塑造黑洞周围混沌粒子动力学中扮演不同但互补的角色，为研究磁化黑洞时空中的非可积粒子运动提供了新框架。

Abstract: We present an exact solution to the Einstein-Maxwell equations that describes a static black hole coexisting with anisotropic matter immersed in an external magnetic field, obtained via the Harrison transformation. Our findings reveal that an increase in the anisotropic matter parameter systematically suppresses the local chaotic behavior, as indicated by a reduction in the Lyapunov exponent. Conversely, variations in the external magnetic field lead to qualitative changes in global chaotic behavior. This is analyzed through Poincaré sections, which demonstrate transitions between regular and chaotic trajectories resulting from the nonlinear gravitational-magnetic interactions. These factors play distinct yet complementary roles in shaping chaotic particle dynamics around black holes. This study would offer a new theoretical framework for exploring non-integrable particle motion within magnetized black hole spacetimes and for probing a black hole at the galactic center, where magnetic fields may arise from plasma effects surrounding astrophysical black holes.

</details>


### [141] [Phase Transitions with Lyapunov Exponents under Einstein and String Frames in Dilatonic Reissner--Nordström--AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01912)
*Hocheol Lee,Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中膨胀的Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞的Lyapunov指数作为相变动力学探针，发现虽然Lyapunov指数本身在共形框架变换下具有依赖性，但其所捕捉的相结构特征（包括尖点行为和转变点）与框架选择无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lyapunov指数能否作为黑洞相变的有效动力学探针，并研究其在共形框架变换下的行为，以建立动力学量与热力学相结构之间的关联。

Method: 在爱因斯坦框架和弦框架下，分析膨胀Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞的热力学量（Hawking温度、Wald熵）和带电探针粒子的Lyapunov指数，通过数值分析比较相结构特征。

Result: 热力学量在共形变换下保持不变，相结构一致；Lyapunov指数在有质量的探针粒子情况下表现出框架依赖性，但在无质量极限下框架依赖性消失；Lyapunov指数捕捉到的相结构特征（尖点行为和转变点）独立于框架选择。

Conclusion: Lyapunov指数虽取值依赖于共形框架，但其揭示的临界结构在不同共形框架下保持恒定，可作为研究黑洞相变的可靠动力学探针。

Abstract: We investigate Lyapunov exponents as dynamical probes of black hole phase transitions in dilatonic Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes within Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton theory. The thermodynamic quantities and the Lyapunov exponent of charged probe particles were analyzed in both the Einstein and string frames, thus providing a direct comparison between the thermodynamic phase structure of the black hole and that captured by the Lyapunov exponent. Thermodynamic quantities, including the Hawking temperature and Wald entropy, remained constant under conformal frame transformations, yielding identical phase structures in the two frames. In contrast, the Lyapunov exponent exhibited non-trivial frame dependence for massive probe particles due to dilaton coupling, while no frame dependence was found in the massless limit. Numerical analysis revealed that the phase structure features captured by the Lyapunov exponent, including characteristic cusp behavior and transition points, were independent of the choice of frame, despite the Lyapunov exponent itself being frame-dependent. Therefore, the Lyapunov exponent exhibited frame-dependent values, while the critical structure it captures remained constant across conformal frames.

</details>


### [142] [Neutron stars with primary scalar hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01937)
*Hamza Boumaza,Christos Charmousis,David Langlois,Etienne Ligout*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在DHOST引力理论子类中研究了具有原初标量毛的静态球对称中子星解，通过求解修正的TOV方程构建了平衡位形，分析了标量毛对星体结构的影响，发现正标量荷导致比广义相对论更致密的恒星，超过临界阈值会产生奇点，观测可对此类超越GR的理论参数施加严格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究DHOST引力理论中静态球对称中子星是否允许携带原初标量毛，并分析标量毛对星体结构（如致密性、质量-半径关系）的影响，以检验超越广义相对论的引力效应。

Method: 求解修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV)方程，采用多方状态方程和 realistic 状态方程构建平衡位形，分析度规和标量场剖面以及质量-半径关系。

Result: 正标量荷导致比GR更致密的中子星；超过临界阈值会出现奇点；质量-半径关系偏离GR预言。

Conclusion: 观测数据可对DHOST理论中超越GR效应的参数及潜在的标量毛施加严格约束。

Abstract: We investigate static and spherically symmetric neutron star solutions endowed with primary scalar hair in a subfamily of Degenerate-Higher-Order-Scalar-Tensor (DHOST) theories of gravity. By solving the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) equations, we construct equilibrium configurations for polytropic and realistic equations of state and analyse the impact of the scalar hair on the stellar structure. We examine the resulting metric and scalar field profiles as well as the mass-radius relation, showing deviations from the predictions of General Relativity (GR). Positive scalar charges lead to more compact stars than in GR and, above a critical threshold, to singularities. Observations could therefore put stringent constraints on the parameters characterising the beyond-GR effects in these theories and their potential scalar hair.

</details>


### [143] [Boson Stars in Teleparallel Gravity with a Nonminimally Coupled Field: The Violation of Energy Conditions and Gravitational Waveforms from EMRIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02017)
*Long-Xing Huang,Ke Yang,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非最小耦合的引力理论中研究玻色子星模型，获得了基态和激发态的静态球对称解。激发态能密度可为负且不满足能量条件，而基态解满足所有能量条件。研究还表明，由这些玻色子星组成的极端质量比旋进系统产生的引力波信号频率特征应变处于LISA探测范围内，可区分致密天体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非最小耦合的引力理论框架下研究玻色子星模型，探索基态与激发态解的物理性质，并评估其作为致密天体候选体的可观测性。

Method: 在非最小耦合的引力理论中建立玻色子星模型，求解静态球对称的基态和激发态数值解，分析能量密度及四种能量条件；进一步模拟由这些玻色子星构成的极端质量比旋进系统的引力波信号。

Result: 激发态解的能量密度可为负，且不满足四种常用能量条件；基态解能量密度为正，满足所有能量条件。引力波信号的频域特征应变在LISA可探测范围内。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合引力中的玻色子星，特别是基态解，具有自洽的物理性质，其EMRI引力波信号可被LISA探测，有助于区分致密天体类型。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate boson star models within the framework of teleparallel gravity with non-minimal coupling, and obtain static, spherically symmetric solutions for both the ground state and excited states. The results indicate that the energy density of the excited-state solutions can become negative. For these solutions, the four commonly used energy conditions are no longer satisfied. In contrast, for all the ground-state solutions we have studied, the energy density remains positive and all four energy conditions are consistently satisfied. Moreover, considering the importance of astrophysical observations, the gravitational-wave signals from Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs) composed of these boson stars are investigated. Our results reveal that the frequency-domain characteristic strain of these waveforms falls within the detectability range of LISA, which can provide potential evidence for distinguishing compact astrophysical objects.

</details>


### [144] [Nonlocal correlations of fermionic entanglement in the spacetime of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02039)
*Yifei Xu,Yanjun Chen,Qi Xiao,Xiaolong Gong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究d维球对称爱因斯坦-高斯-博内黑洞时空中，非局域量子相干优势（NAQC）和贝尔非局域性（BN）两种非经典关联测度的行为。利用Unruh-Hawking效应和Bogoliubov变换，推导了费米子场的混合二分密度矩阵及NAQC和BN的解析表达式。结果表明，两种关联随霍金温度单调退化，NAQC-BN层级关系在EGB时空中依然保持，高曲率修正对量子资源有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弯曲时空中的非经典关联，为量子信息理论与引力物理的交叉领域提供关键见解，特别是在高曲率修正（如EGB引力）背景下量子资源的演化行为。

Method: 考虑Alice（自由落入黑洞，惯性Kruskal坐标系）和Rob（视界外加速，非惯性Schwarzschild-like坐标系）初始共享最大纠缠贝尔态；利用Unruh-Hawking效应修正Rob的场模式，通过Bogoliubov变换关联两个坐标系；推导费米子场的混合二分密度矩阵以及NAQC和BN的解析表达式。

Result: NAQC和BN均随霍金温度升高而单调退化；NAQC-BN之间的层级关系在EGB时空中依然保持；高曲率修正（由参数α、维度d和视界半径r_h控制）对量子资源有显著影响。

Conclusion: 在EGB黑洞时空中，Hawking辐射导致的Unruh效应会破坏量子关联，验证了NAQC与BN的层级关系在弯曲时空中的普适性，并揭示了高曲率修正对量子信息资源的调控作用。

Abstract: The investigation of nonclassical correlations in curved spacetimes offers key insights into the intersection of quantum information theory and gravitational physics. This paper studies two nonlocal correlation measures, non local advantage of quantum coherence (NAQC) and Bell nonlocality (BN) in a $d$-dimensional spherically symmetric Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) black hole spacetime. We consider two observers (Alice and Rob) initially sharing a maximally entangled Bell state: Alice freely falls into the black hole (inertial Kruskal frame), while Rob accelerates outside the horizon (non-inertial Schwarzschild-like frame). The Unruh-Hawking effect modifies Rob's field modes, requiring Bogoliubov transformations to relate the two frames. We derive the mixed bipartite density matrix for fermionic fields and analytical expressions for NAQC and BN, which depend on Hawking temperature (itself governed by $α$, $d$, and $r_h$). Our results show both correlations degrade monotonically with increasing Hawking temperature, confirm the NAQC-BN hierarchical relationship persists in EGB spacetime, and highlighting the impact of high curvature corrections on quantum resources.

</details>


### [145] [The Direct Wave is Not a Meaningful Test of Horizon Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02380)
*Anuj Kankani,Sean T. McWilliams*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论应变数据证明，黑洞并合辐射中的直接波（direct wave）频率与视界频率或表面引力无关，仅在某特定自旋值附近存在偶然交叉；直接波的阻尼时间存在显著演化，单一阻尼正弦模型不适用；基于视界性质的演化频率模型在高自旋系统中失效；若基于直接波解释检验霍金面积定律会导致虚假的违例。结论：直接波不能可靠探测残余视界性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究识别出黑洞并合辐射中的直接波分量，并将其频率和阻尼时间与残余视界性质关联，甚至用于分析GW250114事件及检验霍金面积定律。本文旨在验证直接波与视界性质的真实相关性。

Method: 使用数值相对论应变数据，分析直接波的瞬时频率和阻尼时间演化特性，检验其与视界频率/表面引力的相关性，并评估基于视界性质的演化频率模型在高自旋系统中的适用性。

Result: 直接波频率与视界频率/表面引力无真实相关；阻尼时间存在显著演化，单一阻尼正弦模型不适用；基于视界性质的演化频率模型对大自旋残余系统失效；基于直接波检验霍金面积定律会导致虚假违例。

Conclusion: 直接波不能作为残余视界性质的可靠探针，此前基于直接波的视界性质分析和霍金面积定律检验应当重新审视。

Abstract: Recently, a distinct non--quasinormal mode component of black hole binary merger radiation, named the direct wave, has been identified. The frequency and damping time of the direct wave have been associated with properties of the remnant horizon. This has led to direct-wave based analysis of GW250114, including a test of Hawking's area law. However, as we demonstrate here using numerical relativity strain data, the direct wave frequency is not correlated with the horizon frequency or surface gravity, other than an incidental crossing around $χ_f \approx 0.7$ corresponding closely to the remnant spin of GW250114. We show that while the instantaneous frequency of the direct wave is quasi-stable, the damping time shows significant evolution and therefore a single damped sinusoid model, containing a fixed damping time, is not appropriate. We further show that an evolving frequency model based on horizon properties also does not model the direct wave component for systems with large remnant spins. We demonstrate that testing Hawking's area law with a horizon frequency based on the direct wave interpretation will lead to apparent violations of Hawking's area law when no violation actually occurs. Our results therefore indicate that the direct wave is not a reliable probe of the remnant horizon's properties.

</details>


### [146] [Black Hole Persistence in Scalar Tensor Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02409)
*Balkar Yildirim,Alan Albert Coley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在标量-张量引力框架下，通过微扰方法构建了包含中心不均匀性的反弹宇宙学解，发现一个尺度约$d_0$的演化视界在反弹过程中持续存在，支持黑洞经历宇宙学反弹后仍能存续的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞能否在非奇异宇宙反弹过程中存续，即在经历反弹（收缩-膨胀转变）后中心不均匀性（黑洞）是否仍然存在。

Method: 采用微扰方案（微扰参数$\epsilon$），零阶为含辐射完美流体的平坦FLRW反弹宇宙；一阶引入广义McVittie几何描述的中央不均匀性，通过各向异性流体的对角场方程求解，在反弹点$\eta=0$附近展开至$\mathcal{O}(\eta^4)$，并通过渐近FLRW条件约束自由函数。

Result: 得到了含三个任意函数的完美流体解，积分常数$d_0$（$d_0\to0$时所有微扰消失）作为真正的微扰参数；发现一个尺度$r_h\sim d_0$的小演化视界。

Conclusion: 该演化视界在反弹中持续存在，支持黑洞存活于宇宙学反弹的解读；且视界演化关于$\eta=0$不对称。

Abstract: We construct a perturbative scalar-tensor solution describing a central inhomogeneity embedded in an evolving cosmological background, with the aim of studying black hole persistence through a nonsingular bounce. Scalar-tensor gravity provides a natural framework for realizing bouncing cosmologies, while the inclusion of a localized inhomogeneity makes the field equations substantially more difficult to solve. We therefore adopt a perturbative scheme, with perturbative parameter $ε$, in which the leading-order equations are solved by a spatially flat bouncing FLRW spacetime sourced by a radiation perfect fluid. At next order, a central inhomogeneity is introduced through a generalized McVittie geometry, with the perturbations encoded in the corresponding first-order metric and scalar-field functions. We first allow an anisotropic fluid with radial and tangential pressures, whose diagonal components solve the diagonal field equations. The field equations are solved as a series expansion up to $\mathcal{O}(η^4)$ near the bounce at $η=0$. The resulting perfect fluid solution contains three arbitrary functions which are constrained by requiring the spacetime to asymptote to FLRW as $r\to\infty$. With suitable initial conditions preserving the parabolic structure of the bounce, the integration constant $d_0$ emerges as the true perturbative parameter: all perturbations vanish as $d_0\to0$. Finally, we find a small evolving horizon, $r_h\sim d_0$, which we interpret as the horizon of the central inhomogeneity. Its persistence through the bounce supports the interpretation of a black hole surviving the cosmological transition, and its evolution is not symmetric about $η=0$.

</details>
