### [147] [Can Primordial Black Holes Be Seeds for Early Galaxies in Models Satisfying the Covariant Entropy Bound?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01292)
*Sidan A,Tom Banks,Willy Fischler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证满足协变熵界（CEB）的宇宙学模型在数学上倾向于无局域激发或只有一个大黑洞的初始状态；提出早期宇宙大部分视界体积包含微小黑洞（衰变为辐射）和至少视界大小的随机黑洞（解释暗物质和JWST早期星系），并用广义相对论近似解和量子引力模型描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决协变熵界约束下宇宙早期状态的理论偏好问题，同时解释宇宙微波背景数据、暗物质成分以及詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜观测到的早期星系。

Method: 基于协变熵界的数学约束推导初始状态偏好；提出包含微小原初黑洞（衰变产生辐射）和大尺度原初黑洞（作为暗物质）的宇宙学场景；用广义相对论近似解和饱和CEB的平坦p=±ρ FRW模型的量子引力流体力学进行建模。

Result: 满足CEB的模型在数学上偏好无激发态或单个大黑洞的初始条件；通过原初黑洞分布可同时解释CMB辐射、全部暗物质以及JWST观测到的早期星系；部分暗物质可能来自黑洞衰变后的普朗克尺度残余。

Conclusion: 提出一个以原初黑洞为核心的自洽宇宙学框架，在满足协变熵界的前提下同时解释了辐射主导时代、暗物质和早期星系等观测现象。

Abstract: We argue that cosmological models obeying the Covariant Entropy Bound (CEB) mathematically favor states with no localized excitations or one large black hole containing all the energy in a constrained initial state. In order to get a long radiation-dominated era, one must postulate that at a very early time, most horizon volumes of the universe contained tiny black holes that decayed into radiation. A previous work by two of the authors showed that such a scenario could fit the data on the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB). In order to account for dark matter, we also postulate some random black holes of at least horizon size at that time. A reasonable distribution of such primordial black holes can account for all of dark matter as well as the early galaxies seen by the James Webb Space Telescope. Some of the dark matter may also be in Planck-scale remnants of the decaying black holes. We describe our model both in terms of approximate solutions to General Relativity and a speculative quantum gravity model whose hydrodynamics matches the flat $p = \pm ρ$ FRW model that saturates the CEB.

</details>


### [148] [No Evidence for Superradiant Axions in LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-5 Binary Black Hole Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01317)
*Orion Ning,Benjamin R. Safdi,Catherine Welch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-5目录中257个并合黑洞的自旋测量数据，通过分层贝叶斯分析，在95%置信水平上排除了轴子质量范围1.7×10⁻¹⁴ eV ≲ m_a ≲ 3.3×10⁻¹² eV，为QCD轴子质量提供了最强健的下限之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻轴子（包括QCD轴子）可通过超辐射机制在旋转黑洞周围形成束缚云，消耗黑洞自旋。利用引力波观测到的双黑洞自旋数据，可以约束轴子的存在及其质量范围。

Method: 使用GWTC-5目录中257个双黑洞并合事件的自旋测量数据（黑洞质量5-135 M⊙），构建黑洞自旋种群模型，通过分层贝叶斯分析检验轴子存在与否对质量-自旋关系的印记。

Result: 未发现轴子存在的证据，在95%置信水平上排除轴子质量1.7×10⁻¹⁴ eV ≲ m_a ≲ 3.3×10⁻¹² eV（跨越两个数量级以上）。

Conclusion: 该结果是对QCD轴子质量的最强健下限之一，优于此前依赖X射线自旋测量（存在较大建模系统误差）的约束。

Abstract: The quantum chromodynamics (QCD) axion and axion-like particles may form bound clouds around spinning black holes (BHs) when their Compton wavelength is comparable to the BH gravitational radius, depleting the BH spin through what is known as a $\textit{superradiance}$ instability. Using binary BH (BBH) spin measurements obtained from the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA GWTC-5 catalog, the most extensive public BBH catalog to date containing $N=257$ mergers with BH masses spanning roughly $5$-$135$ $M_\odot$, we perform a hierarchical Bayesian analysis in the context of a BH spin population model to constrain ultralight axions. The presence of axions at a given mass would imprint a unique signature in the observed mass-spin relation relative to the formation distribution. We find no evidence for axions across more than two decades in mass, excluding axion masses $1.7 \times 10^{-14} \, {\rm eV} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 3.3 \times 10^{-12} \, {\rm eV}$ at 95% confidence. Because prior superradiance bounds in this range derive from X-ray spin measurements with substantial modeling systematics, this result represents one of the strongest robust lower bounds on the QCD axion mass.

</details>


### [149] [Axion Misalignment Across First-Order Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01333)
*Galymzhan Baltabay,Francesco D'Eramo,Ville Vaskonen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子质量在一阶相变中产生时的暗物质产生机制。通过晶格模拟发现两个不同区域：快速相变延迟振荡开始增强遗迹丰度，慢速相变通过气泡错位机制抑制有效错位角。推导了半解析表达式统一描述两个区域，并展示了该机制对等曲率扰动和小尺度物质功率谱的修改，影响轴子微团簇形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准错位产生图像在轴子质量仅存在于膨胀真空气泡内部时会被定性修改，因此需要研究这种新框架下的暗物质产生机制。

Method: 在膨胀宇宙中进行晶格模拟，研究不同相变速率下的暗物质产生；推导半解析表达式描述遗迹密度。

Result: 识别出快速和慢速相变两个不同区域，分别对应遗迹丰度增强和抑制；半解析表达式准确重现模拟结果；该机制还修改了等曲率扰动和小尺度物质功率谱。

Conclusion: 轴子质量通过一阶相变产生的情景会定性改变标准错位产生图像，对轴子暗物质的遗迹丰度和微团簇形成有重要影响。

Abstract: When the axion mass is generated during a first-order phase transition and becomes non-vanishing only inside expanding true-vacuum bubbles, the standard picture of misalignment production is qualitatively modified. Using lattice simulations in an expanding universe, we study dark matter production within such a framework and identify two distinct regimes. For rapid transitions, the onset of oscillations is delayed until bubble percolation, enhancing the relic abundance. For slower transitions, spatial gradients generated by expanding bubbles suppress the effective misalignment angle through the bubble misalignment mechanism. We derive a semi-analytical expression for the relic density that provides a unified description of both regimes and accurately reproduces the simulation results. Finally, we show how this mechanism also modifies isocurvature perturbations and the small-scale matter power spectrum, with important implications for axion minicluster formation.

</details>


### [150] [Electron stability constrains neutrino time delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01339)
*Mauricio Bustamante,José Manuel Carmona,José Luis Cortés,Ardit Gkioni,Maykoll A. Reyes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出，由洛伦兹不变性破缺（LIV）引起的中微子超光速传播受到真空对发射的强约束，而亚光速传播则面临先前被忽视的电子衰变通道的稳定性限制。通过分析TeV-PeV级天体物理电子的观测数据，发现电子稳定性限排除了用LIV解释高能宇宙中微子延迟的可能性。因此，中微子时间延迟只能由纯天体物理起源、对所有粒子同等影响的LIV、或超越标准有效场论框架的物理来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为LIV导致的中微子超光速传播受真空对发射约束，亚光速传播则更可能解释观测到的中微子-伽马射线时间延迟。然而，作者注意到这一预期与高能电子观测稳定性相矛盾，因为亚光速中微子传播对应的LIV修正应该也会打开电子的互补衰变通道，从而引起电子不稳定性。因此，需要检验这一被忽略的约束。

Method: 推导亚光速中微子LIV情形下电子衰变通道（e⁻ → e⁻ + ν + ν̄）的约束条件，并利用TeV-PeV级天体物理电子的观测数据来限制LIV参数空间。

Result: 电子稳定性限排除了此前用LIV解释高能宇宙中微子时间延迟的理论方案。中微子时间延迟在超光速和亚光速两侧均受到约束。

Conclusion: 可观测的中微子时间延迟要么需要纯天体物理起源，要么需要一种对所有粒子种类同等影响的LIV实现方式，或者需要超越标准有效场论框架的新物理。

Abstract: Superluminal neutrino propagation, induced by Lorentz-invariance violation (LIV), is strongly constrained by vacuum pair emission, $ν\to ν+ e^- + e^+$, a process ordinarily forbidden, which rapidly degrades the energy of high-energy neutrinos. Consequently, observable neutrino time delays are often preferentially associated with subluminal propagation, prompting LIV interpretations of claimed time delays between high-energy cosmic neutrinos and gamma rays. However, this expectation is at odds with the observed stability of high-energy electrons. The same Lorentz-violating correction associated with subluminal neutrino propagation opens the overlooked complementary decay channel $e^- \to e^- + ν+ \barν$, leading to electron instability. We derive constraints on LIV from recent observations of TeV--PeV astrophysical electrons. These electron stability limits rule out LIV invoked to explain delays of high-energy cosmic neutrinos. Consequently, neutrino time delays are constrained on both the superluminal and subluminal sides. Therefore, observable delays require either purely astrophysical origins, a realization of LIV that affects all particle species equally, or physics beyond the standard effective-field-theory framework.

</details>


### [151] [Local Conformal Predictions for Calibrated Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01354)
*Suprio Dubey,Henning Bahl,Anja Butter,Jürgen Hesser,Tilman Plehn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON方法，利用保形预测为LHC散射幅度的神经网络代理模型提供分布无关、局部校准的不确定性估计，解决了标准保形预测在非高斯系统噪声下局部校准不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC散射幅度的神经网络代理模型需要可靠的不确定性估计，但非高斯系统噪声使得传统方法难以实现局部校准的置信区间。

Method: 提出FALCON，一种基于保形预测（conformal prediction）的新方法，通过学习局部校准的置信区间来对训练好的代理模型进行后处理。

Result: FALCON能够提供局部校准的不确定性估计，克服了标准保形预测在此类问题上的局限性；通过简单示例展示了分布无关不确定性量化的有效性。

Conclusion: 分布无关的不确定性量化方法（如FALCON）对于LHC超快事件生成中的代理模型校准具有重要价值。

Abstract: Neural network surrogates for LHC scattering amplitudes require trustworthy uncertainty estimates, a challenging task given the non-Gaussian systematics. We target it using conformal prediction, a distribution-free post-processing to complement trained surrogates with calibrated uncertainties. We find that standard conformal predictions struggle to provide locally calibrated uncertainties. This leads us to introduce FALCON, a novel conformal prediction method that learns locally calibrated confidence intervals. Our simple examples illustrate the power of distribution-free uncertainty quantification for ultra-fast event generation at the LHC.

</details>


### [152] [Neutron stars as thermometers for reheating induced dipole dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01390)
*Sahabub Jahedi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论框架下研究了偶极子暗物质的电磁相互作用，考虑了标准宇宙学和非标准宇宙学两种场景。分析了冻结和冻结-in两种暗物质产生机制，探讨了重加热期间熵稀释对参数空间的影响，并评估了未来直接探测实验和中子星加热对偶极子暗物质的探测潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索偶极子暗物质在不同宇宙学场景下的产生机制和探测可能性，填补现有研究中对非标准宇宙学背景下偶极子暗物质参数空间约束的不足。

Method: 采用有效场论框架，分别研究标准辐射主导时期下的冻结和冻结-in产生机制；考虑非标准宇宙学中重加热阶段的熵稀释效应；利用中子星加热作为动量依赖相互作用的灵敏探针。

Result: 偶极子暗物质的参数空间在标准和非标准宇宙学场景下存在显著差异；中子星加热对偶极子暗物质具有高灵敏度的探测能力；未来直接探测实验有望覆盖更广泛的参数空间区域。

Conclusion: 偶极子暗物质的探测需要结合多种手段，中子星加热和直接探测实验相互补充，非标准宇宙学显著扩展了可行的参数空间区域。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic interactions of dipole dark matter (DM) within an effective field theory framework, considering both standard and non-standard cosmological scenarios. We first study the prospects of DM production via both the freeze-out and freeze-in mechanisms within the standard radiation-domination. We then investigate how the viable DM parameter space is modified in a non-standard cosmological scenario due to entropy dilution during reheating. Existing constraints on the parameter space are discussed, and we highlight the discovery potential of future direct detection experiments to probe these scenarios. We further investigate the implications of neutron star heating for dipole DM. Due to the momentum-dependent nature of the interaction, dipole DM is captured efficiently by neutron stars, thereby making neutron star heating a sensitive probe of the dipole DM parameter space.

</details>


### [153] [One-loop matching of QCD currents to power-suppressed two-jet operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01413)
*Martin Beneke,Aleksey V. Rusov,Michel Stillger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SCET中首次计算了QCD夸克-反夸克流到两粒子和三粒子两喷注算符的匹配，包括到NLO和横向动量/能量展开的次领头阶，并证明了端点因子化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进对事件形状（两喷注区域）和Bjorken-x→1极限下深度非弹性散射的幂次修正计算以及幂次增强对数项的求和。

Method: 在SCET框架下，在NLO微扰QCD水平上计算QCD流到两粒子和三粒子两喷注算符的匹配，首次包含横向动量/能量展开到二阶的算符，并分析动量分数趋于零时的端点因子化关系。

Result: 得到了NLO匹配系数；发现在动量分数趋于零时系数函数满足端点因子化关系，可实现完整因子化公式中端点奇异性的相消。

Conclusion: 这些结果为系统计算两喷注区域和DIS端点区域的幂次修正及求和增强对数项提供了关键输入。

Abstract: We compute the matching of QCD quark-antiquark currents onto the set of the two-particle and three-particle two-jet operators in soft-collinear effective theory (SCET) at next-to-leading order (NLO) in the perturbative QCD series, including for the first time operators up to second order in the power expansion in the transverse momentum over energy. These results contribute to the ongoing programme of computing power corrections and summing power-suppressed logarithmically enhanced terms for event shapes in the two-jet region and deep-inelastic scattering in the Bjorken-$x\to 1$ limit. The three-particle operators depend on the partonic momentum fractions of two partons moving into the same direction. When one of the momentum fractions approaches zero, the coefficient functions are shown to satisfy endpoint factorization relations, which allow for a consistent cancellation of endpoint singularities among various terms in the complete factorization formula for power corrections.

</details>


### [154] [The theory of electric dipole moments: the view from below](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01443)
*Jordy de Vries*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文从自下而上的角度系统回顾了永久电偶极矩(EDM)理论，阐述了从夸克-胶子层次的CP破坏有效拉格朗日量，通过手征微扰论、核子与核EDM及Schiff矩计算，最终连接到原子分子可观测量的完整链条，并特别讨论了顺磁系统对强子CP破坏的敏感性及其在整个EDM组合中的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDM是探测超标准模型CP破坏最灵敏的探针之一，与强CP问题和宇宙正反物质不对称性起源密切相关。本文旨在通过自下而上的理论框架，建立从基本粒子CP破坏相互作用到可观测EDM之间的完整联系链条，为解读不同实验系统之间的互补性提供理论基础。

Method: 采用自下而上的理论方法：从夸克-胶子层次的一般CP奇有效拉格朗日量出发（包含QCD theta项、夸克EDM和色EDM、Weinberg算符、CP奇四费米子相互作用），利用手征微扰论组织非微扰QCD动力学，得到一组强子低能常数；进而计算核EDM和Schiff矩，最后通过原子分子结构计算连接实验可观测量。

Result: 阐明了从基本粒子CP破坏到可观测EDM的完整理论链条；指出顺磁系统对强子CP破坏具有敏感性，为探索夸克-胶子扇区开辟了新窗口；展示了中子、轻核、原子和分子等完整EDM组合的互补性。

Conclusion: EDM实验组合与理论的结合能够有效区分不同CP破坏来源，顺磁系统的敏感性为探索强子CP破坏提供了新的途径，整个EDM组合的互补性对于揭示CP破坏的起源至关重要。

Abstract: Permanent electric dipole moments (EDMs) of nucleons, nuclei, atoms, and molecules are among the most sensitive probes of CP violation beyond the Standard Model and are intimately connected to the strong CP problem and the origin of the matter-antimatter asymmetry of the universe. This review presents the theory of EDMs from the bottom up, tracing the chain of connections that links CP-violating interactions at level of elementary particles to observable EDMs across a wide range of systems. Starting from a general CP-odd effective Lagrangian at the quark-gluon level comprising the QCD theta term, quark EDMs and chromo-EDMs, the Weinberg operator, and CP-odd four-fermion interactions, I show how chiral perturbation theory organizes the nonperturbative QCD dynamics into a small set of hadronic low-energy constants, whose relative sizes are determined by the chiral representation of the underlying source. These hadronic interactions feed into calculations of nuclear EDMs and Schiff moments, which in turn enter atomic and molecular structure calculations that connect to experimentally accessible observables in diamagnetic and paramagnetic systems. Special attention is given to the recently identified sensitivity of paramagnetic systems to hadronic CP violation, which opens a new and relatively unexplored window on the quark-gluon sector. The complementarity of the full EDM portfolio including the neutron, light nuclei, atoms, and molecules, and the role of theory in disentangling the underlying source of CP violation is discussed throughout.

</details>


### [155] [An introduction to Hammer v2: Helicity Amplitude Module for Matrix Element Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01459)
*Michele Papucci,Dean J. Robinson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Hammer软件库v2.x版本实现了对包含半轻子b-强子衰变的大型模拟数据集进行快速高效的重加权，支持任意BSM理论或形状因子描述，计算复杂度从四次方优化为近线性，并提供了完整的Python绑定和API文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实验数据分析中，探测器模拟计算开销巨大，传统方法需要在模拟阶段固定理论模型。Hammer旨在解决这一问题，允许在模拟完成后重加权到不同的理论模型，从而支持forward-folding拟合策略以无偏恢复物理参数或正确表征理论系统不确定性。

Method: 通过优化内部张量库，将计算复杂度从振幅张量秩乘以尺寸的四次方降低到近线性规模；更新了API和Python绑定（与C++接口双射对应）；支持BSM Wilson系数与形状因子参数线性空间的乘积等高维空间的重加权，同时保持蒙特卡洛不确定性。

Result: 实现了在已完成探测器模拟的数据集上快速重加权到不同理论模型，计算复杂度大幅降低，支持高维参数空间（如BSM Wilson系数×形状因子参数）的重加权，Python绑定提供完整功能访问。

Conclusion: Hammer v2.x通过张量库优化和API更新，使得实验分析能够在模拟后灵活重加权至不同理论模型，支持前向拟合策略和系统不确定性表征，显著提升了重加权在高维空间中的计算效率。

Abstract: The Hammer software library provides fast and efficient reweighting of large simulated datasets containing semileptonic $b$-hadron decays to any beyond Standard Model (BSM) theory, or to any form-factor description of the hadronic matrix elements. By enabling reweighting to a different underlying theoretical model after the computationally-expensive detector simulation step has already been completed, Hammer permits experimental analyses to employ forward-folding fitting strategies to recover underlying physical parameters without biases, or to properly characterize theory systematic uncertainties. This publication details upgrades to Hammer functionalities and its application programming interface (API) for version 2.x, and also provides associated documentation of the library's structure, syntactical conventions, and code flow. Substantial optimization of Hammer's internal tensor library now enables computational complexity to generically scale almost linearly with amplitude tensor rank times size rather than as a quartic, enabling reweighting into very high dimension spaces, such as the product of BSM Wilson coefficient and form factor parameter linear spaces, while also retaining Monte Carlo uncertainties. Updated Python bindings are implemented with bijective correspondence to the C++ interface, allowing full access to library functionalities.

</details>


### [156] [Hadronization of $Λ_c^+$ baryons from recombination model in Pb+Pb collisions at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01637)
*Jing-Zong Zhang,Hua Zheng,Lilin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在夸克重组框架下研究了Pb+Pb碰撞中$\Lambda_c^+$重子的产生，同时描述了横动量谱、$\Lambda_c^+/D^0$产额比和方位角各向异性，验证了重组模型是重离子碰撞中有效的强子化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重离子碰撞中，$\Lambda_c^+$重子的产生机制尚不完全清楚，特别是夸克重组机制在QGP中相对于碎裂机制的贡献需要进一步验证。

Method: 基于夸克重组框架，考虑了轻夸克和粲夸克在热密介质中的能量损失，同时引入了minijet对方位角各向异性的影响。

Result: 模型同时描述了$\Lambda_c^+$重子的横动量谱、$\Lambda_c^+/D^0$产额比（归因于QGP中夸克重组机制的主导地位），以及$\Lambda_c^+$重子的二阶谐波系数。还将计算拓展到2.76 TeV并给出了预测。

Conclusion: 对产额、重子-介子比和方位角各向异性的同时描述进一步验证了重组模型是重离子碰撞中有效的强子化机制。

Abstract: The production of $Λ_c^+$ baryons in Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02$ TeV is investigated within the quark recombination framework, including the energy loss of light and charm quarks inside the hot and dense medium. The model simultaneously describes the transverse momentum ($p_T$) spectra of $Λ_c^+$ baryons, $Λ_c^+/D^0$ yield ratio, which is attributed to the dominance of quark recombination mechanism in the Quark-Gluon Plasma (QGP), and the second harmonic coefficient of $Λ_c^+$ baryons with emphasis on the effects of minijets on the azimuthal anisotropy. Furthermore, we extend the theoretical calculation to Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} =2.76$ TeV and make predictions for $Λ_c^+$ baryons and $Λ_c^+/D^0$ yield ratio. The simultaneous description of the yield, baryon-to-meson ratio, and azimuthal anisotropy further validates that the recombination model is an effective hadronization mechanism in heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [157] [Gravitational Waves from Multiple First-Order Phase Transitions in a Scenario with Early Matter Domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01697)
*Rouzbeh Allahverdi,Fazlollah Hajkarim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了早期物质主导（EMD）宇宙学中非标准的热历史，重点关注与时间相关的衰变率如何导致非单调温度演化，从而在冷却和加热阶段产生多次一级相变，并产生具有多峰和特殊高频行为的引力波信号，为探测新物理和早期热历史提供了窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学假设辐射主导时期，但许多高能模型（如弦理论、超引力等）预测存在早期物质主导（EMD）阶段。EMD后期因驱动物质的衰变产生熵，导致温度下降比辐射主导更慢。而时间相关的衰变率可能进一步改变这一图像，甚至导致温度上升。研究这种非单调温度演化及其可观测后果具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 研究了一个具有时间依赖衰变率的EMD场景，分析了该场景下温度的非单调演化，并研究了由此产生的多次一级相变（包括冷却和加热阶段）。进一步计算了由此产生的引力波谱，并分析了其特征。

Result: 发现该EMD场景可产生多个一级相变，对应的引力波谱呈现多峰结构和高频区的特殊行为。这些特征可用于确定相变温度以及EMD结束时的再加热温度。

Conclusion: 未来的引力波探测器（如LISA、DECIGO等）有望探测到这些特征信号，从而为研究早期宇宙的非标准热历史和新物理提供新的探针。

Abstract: Non-standard cosmological histories with epochs of early matter domination (EMD) arise in various top-down models of the early universe. Typically, in the latter stage of EMD, temperature decreases more slowly than in a radiation dominated universe because of entropy generation from decay of the species that drives EMD. A time-dependent decay rate can significantly modify this picture and even lead to a period with increasing temperature. We study non-monotonic temperature evolution in a well-motivated scenario of EMD with a time-dependent decay rate that can give rise to multiple first-order phase transitions in both cooling and heating phases. The spectra of the ensuing gravitational waves (GW) exhibit characteristic features such as multiple peaks and a distinct behavior at high frequencies. These features allow us to determine the phase transition temperature as well as the reheating temperature at the end of the EMD. The future GW detectors can therefore provide a probe for the new physics and a window to the early thermal history.

</details>


### [158] [Subensemble Acceptance Method 3.0: General Corrections to Cumulants from Exact Conservation Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01783)
*Roman Poberezhniuk,Volodymyr A. Kuznietsov,Grégoire Pihan,Volodymyr Vovchenko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: SAM-3.0是一种修正子系统测量中全局守恒效应的形式化方法，通过基于多元偏指数Bell多项式的封闭递归，从联合巨正则累积量推导出正则累积量，适用于任意数量可观测量和守恒荷，包含SAM-1.0和SAM-2.0作为特例，并精确再现二项式接受极限。在RHIC-BES能量下更新了净质子累积量的非临界基线，与直接正则蒙特卡罗采样一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型系统的子系统中测量可观测量时，由于全局守恒荷的精确守恒，子系统测量结果会产生偏差。需要一种系统的方法来修正这种效应，以获得正确的正则系综累积量。

Method: 提出SAM-3.0框架，基于多元偏指数Bell多项式的封闭递归，从联合巨正则累积量代数推导出正则累积量。该方法通过鞍点展开导出有限尺寸修正的主导阶项，并支持任意数量的可观测量和同时守恒的荷（包括总能量，对应微正则系综）。

Result: SAM-3.0包含SAM-1.0和SAM-2.0作为特例，并精确再现二项式接受极限。应用于RHIC-BES能量下的净质子累积量，更新了基于流体动力学的非临界基线（Hydro-EV），结果与直接正则蒙特卡罗采样一致，且接近SAM-2.0的结果。通过直接蒙特卡罗采样验证了重子数、电荷和奇异数同时守恒下的有效性，包含强子末态效应。

Conclusion: SAM-3.0是修正子系统测量中全局守恒效应的通用且精确的形式化方法，为高能重离子碰撞中的净质子累积量分析提供了更可靠的基线。

Abstract: We present the subensemble acceptance method 3.0 (SAM-3.0), which corrects cumulants of an observable measured in a subsystem of a large system for the effect of exact global conservation of multiple charges. The required input is the set of joint grand-canonical cumulants of the acceptance observable with the total event charges, from which the canonical cumulants follow algebraically via a closed recursion based on (multivariate) partial exponential Bell polynomials. The framework accommodates any number of observables, including non-conserved quantities such as net protons, and any number of simultaneously conserved charges, including the total energy, which yields the microcanonical ensemble. The mapping contains SAM-1.0 and SAM-2.0 as special cases and, unlike SAM-2.0, reproduces the exact binomial-acceptance limit. We also derive the leading finite-size corrections from the saddle-point expansion. We apply the method to update the hydrodynamics-based non-critical baseline (Hydro-EV) for net-proton cumulants at RHIC-BES energies, finding a refined baseline that agrees with direct canonical Monte Carlo sampling and stays close to the earlier SAM-2.0 result. We further validate the formalism against direct Monte Carlo sampling with exact simultaneous conservation of baryon number, electric charge, and strangeness, including hadronic-afterburner effects.

</details>


### [159] [Hybrid Residual Correction of VMC Charmonium Masses with a Screened Funnel Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01797)
*Tarik Akan,Metin Yalvac*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QCD势模型框架下，将变分蒙特卡洛(VMC)与神经网络残差校正相结合，对粲夸克偶素质量进行计算。该方法将ML作为势模型谱学的残差校正层，将七个实验验证态的MAE从438.1 MeV降至24.1 MeV（降低94.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估机器学习校正（NN残差校正器）在改进基于同一屏蔽漏斗势的VMC基线方面的有效性，而非提出新的粲夸克偶素谱。

Method: 采用VMC变分法和确定性特征值对角化进行质量计算，生成大量构型和局域能量估计值，输入神经网络残差校正器学习原始物理基线（VMC结果）与内部参考目标之间的残差差异。

Result: 计算十个粲夸克偶素态，其中七个使用实验参考质量。校正后MAE从438.1 MeV降至24.1 MeV，降幅达94.5%。

Conclusion: ML可以作为势模型谱学的有效残差校正层，显著提升计算精度。

Abstract: In this study, we combine residual correction with the physical treatment of charmonium masses within a Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) motivated potential-model framework via variational Monte Carlo (VMC). The aim is not to propose a new charmonium spectrum, since this sector has already been examined extensively through different potential models. Instead, the main objective is to evaluate how effectively a Machine Learning (ML) correction can improve a VMC baseline when both stages are built on the same screened funnel potential. In this workflow, the screened interaction provides the physical input and determines the underlying mass structure. The proposed run uses the variational method via VMC and deterministic eigenvalue diagonalization in the process of mass calculation. In total, ten charmonium states are calculated, among which seven use the experimental reference masses. The VMC step generates a large set of configurations and local energy estimators that are fed into the neural network (NN) residual corrector at the sample level, while the corrections and experimental uncertainties are collected at the state level. It then learns the residual difference between the raw physics baseline and the internal reference targets. This hybrid procedure reduces the systematic mass offset of the raw calculation across the studied states. For the experimentally verified seven states, the correction reduces the MAE from $438.1~\mathrm{MeV}$ to $24.1~\mathrm{MeV}$, corresponding to a $94.5\%$ reduction. These results show that ML can serve as a residual-correction layer for potential-model spectroscopy.

</details>


### [160] [Phenomenology of genuine twist-three distributions from a global QCD analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01866)
*Guillermo Portela,Alexey Vladimirov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过全局QCD分析，首次系统提取了 genuine twist-three 部分子分布函数，验证了其在DIS和SIDIS过程中的普适性，并构建了质子断层成像图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 twist-three PDFs 的研究分散于不同过程，缺乏统一的全局分析来验证其普适性和QCD因子化定理在次领头阶的有效性。

Method: 基于结构函数 $g_2$、矩 $d_2$ 以及SIDIS中单/双自旋不对称性的全局QCD分析，同时提取了Sivers和worm-gear-T横向动量依赖（TMD）分布。

Result: 确认了genuine twist-three PDFs的普适性，验证了QCD因子化定理在次领头阶的有效性，得到了部分子的平均横向动量等物理量，并构建了质子的断层成像图。

Conclusion: 该研究为 twist-three PDFs 提供了统一的全局提取框架，其一致性验证支持了QCD因子化定理的适用性，且副产品TMD分布为质子内部结构提供了更全面的图像。

Abstract: We present an extraction and phenomenological study of genuine twist-three parton distribution functions (PDFs) through a global QCD analysis of the structure function $g_2$ in deep-inelastic scattering (DIS), its moment $d_2$, and single-/double-spin asymmetries in semi-inclusive DIS (SIDIS). The unified description of these processes clearly confirms the universality of genuine twist-three PDFs and the validity of QCD factorization theorems at this subleading level. The extraction is accompanied by several validation tests, tests of sum rules, comparisons with earlier literature, computation forces and average transverse momentum of partons. As a by-product, we also obtain Sivers and worm-gear-T transverse momentum dependent (TMD) distributions, which allows us to construct a tomographic picture of the proton based on substantially broader data.

</details>


### [161] [Refined Sensitivity Estimates for Single-Molecule Magnet Dark Matter Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01868)
*Andrew Eberhardt,Tomoya Fukui,Ryosuke Takehara,Ryotaro Ohno,Yuta Mizukami,Kenichiro Hashimoto,Takanori Fukushima,Shigeki Matsumoto,Tom Melia,Kouki Nozaki,Surjeet Rajendran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了单分子磁体（SMM）晶体作为低质量暗物质探测器的灵敏度。通过考虑自旋弛豫的随机性质，修正了此前过于保守的判据，发现即使热扩散较快时，部分自旋弛豫释放的塞曼能量也能显著降低磁雪崩形成的能量阈值。模拟和两种代表性SMM晶体的低温热性质实验验证支持了这一新认识，推动了SMM基暗物质探测器的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进单分子磁体晶体作为暗物质探测器的灵敏度估计。此前基于"自旋弛豫时间严格小于热扩散时间"的保守判据忽略了自旋弛豫的随机性质，导致对能量阈值的估计过于偏高，限制了探测器对低质量暗物质的敏感度。

Method: 推导了考虑自旋弛豫随机性的解析估计，计算了在扩散较快时仍然发生弛豫的自旋比例，以及这部分自旋释放的塞曼能量对局域加热的贡献；通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论结果；对Mn₁₂-acetate和Mn₃₂两种代表性SMM晶体进行了低温热性质实验测量。

Result: 修正后的判据表明，即使热扩散快于自旋弛豫，仍有一定比例的自旋发生弛豫，其释放的塞曼能量足以在更低能量阈值下触发磁雪崩；模拟结果与解析估计一致；实验验证了两种SMM晶体在低温下具有预期的热性质。

Conclusion: 这项研究为基于SMM晶体的"磁气泡室"暗物质探测器提供了更准确的灵敏度预测，降低了有效能量阈值，扩展了对低质量暗物质的探测能力，并向实现具有可控材料特性的SMM探测器迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: We revisit the sensitivity of Single Molecule Magnet (SMM) crystals as detectors for low-mass dark matter. In previous work, we established the concept of the ``magnetic bubble chamber'', where energy deposited by dark matter triggers a magnetic avalanche in a metastable crystal. The original sensitivity estimates relied on a conservative criterion requiring the spin relaxation time to be strictly shorter than the thermal diffusion time. Here, we demonstrate that this criterion effectively ignores the stochastic nature of spin relaxation. We derive a refined analytic estimate which accounts for the fraction of spins that relax even when diffusion is fast. We show that the Zeeman energy released by this fraction contributes to local heating, significantly lowering the energy threshold for avalanche formation. We present simulation results confirming this effect and report on experimental verification of the assumed low-temperature thermal properties of two representative SMM crystals, Mn$_{12}$-acetate and Mn$_{32}$. Together, these efforts extend this pathfinder program toward the realization of SMM-based detectors with controlled material properties and enhanced dark matter sensitivity.

</details>


### [162] [Dynamics of Self-Interacting Dark Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01920)
*Esau Cervantes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究早期宇宙中通过冻结机制产生的自相互作用暗扇区的动力学，重点关注2↔3类型自数改变反应及其对热历史和现象学的影响，涵盖多种理论实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自相互作用暗扇区中冻结产生机制与2↔3自数改变反应对暗物质热历史和现象学的修改，以及相变动力学与化学平衡之间的关联。

Method: 通过求解耦合玻尔兹曼方程描述数密度和温度的演化，同时考虑冻结产生和同类相食（cannibal）相互作用；研究标量理论、自相互作用暗物质耦合不稳定中介子、非瞬时再加热场景以及隐藏U(1)规范扇区。

Result: 参数空间在某些实现中受到强约束，某些实现中几乎不可见，另一些实现中可能被未来实验探测到；连续能量注入可诱导逆一级相变并暂时恢复对称相。

Conclusion: 不同理论实现导致截然不同的现象学约束和探测前景，隐藏扇区中相变动力学与化学平衡过程相互关联。

Abstract: This thesis investigates the dynamics of self-interacting dark sectors in the early Universe populated through the freeze-in mechanism. The main focus is on scenarios with self-number-changing reactions of the form $2\leftrightarrow3$, and on how these interactions modify the thermal history and phenomenology of the dark sector. Several realizations are studied, including scalar theories with $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and $\mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetries, self-interacting dark matter coupled to an unstable mediator, and cannibal dark matter production during non-instantaneous reheating. The thermal evolution is obtained by solving coupled Boltzmann equations for the relevant number densities and temperatures, accounting for both freeze-in production and cannibal interactions. The resulting parameter space can be strongly constrained, effectively invisible, or potentially accessible to future searches, depending on the realization considered. Finally, the thesis studies a hidden U(1) gauge sector populated via freeze-in, where continuous energy injection can induce an inverse first-order phase transition and temporarily restore the symmetric phase, linking phase-transition dynamics and chemical equilibration in hidden sectors.

</details>


### [163] [One-loop proton decay from Peccei-Quinn symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02026)
*H. B. Câmara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将标准模型中的偶然B+L对称性提升为Peccei-Quinn (PQ)对称性，通过矢量类夸克和标量中介子在自发PQ破缺后残留的Z₂对称性禁止树级质子衰变，但在单圈级通过有效算子u_R u_R d_R e_R诱导质子衰变，同时提供Kim-Shifman-Vainshtein-Zakharov型轴子解决强CP问题。该框架预测了p→e⁺π⁰和p→e⁺π⁰a两种质子衰变通道，并对轴子-光子耦合有明确预言，可在日光望远镜和暗物质实验中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将标准模型的偶然B+L对称性提升为PQ对称性，同时实现自发质子衰变，以解决强CP问题并探索质子不稳定性。

Method: 通过引入矢量类夸克(VLQs)和标量中介子，在PQ自发破缺后残留Z₂对称性禁止树级质子衰变，但在单圈级通过有效算子u_R u_R d_R e_R诱导质子衰变。研究不同的紫外完备方案，分析轴子-光子耦合及质子衰变通道。

Result: 预言了两种质子衰变通道：p→e⁺π⁰和p→e⁺π⁰a（带轴子末态），后者被PQ破缺能标抑制。不同紫外完备模型对轴子-光子耦合有不同预言，可在日光望远镜和暗物质实验中被检验。

Conclusion: 该框架将质子衰变与轴子物理联系起来，提供了可检验的唯象预言，包括质子衰变模式和轴子-光子耦合，并讨论了轴子暗物质在暴胀前后宇宙学中的影响。

Abstract: We promote the accidental $B+L$ symmetry of the Standard Model to a Peccei-Quinn (PQ) symmetry while realizing spontaneous proton decay radiatively. The PQ anomaly sector consists of vector-like quarks (VLQs), providing a Kim-Shifman-Vainshtein-Zakharov-type axion solution to the strong CP problem. After spontaneous PQ breaking, a residual $\mathcal{Z}_2$ symmetry remains, which forbids tree-level proton decay. The VLQs required to generate the QCD anomaly, together with scalar mediators, odd under the residual $\mathcal{Z}_2$, induce one-loop proton decay through the effective operator $u_R u_R d_R e_R$. We study its ultraviolet completions, featuring distinct vector-like fermion and scalar representations, and show that the resulting models lead to distinct predictions for the axion-to-photon coupling, testable in helioscope and haloscope experiments. In addition to the proton decay channel $p \rightarrow e^+ π^0$, this framework predicts proton decay with an axion in the final state, $p \rightarrow e^+ π^0 a$, suppressed by the PQ-breaking scale. We also discuss axion dark matter in pre- and post-inflationary cosmology.

</details>


### [164] [$Λ$ hyperons in core-collapse supernovae: Equilibration and neutrino opacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02086)
*Ruben Zatini,Jorge Martin Camalich,Pasquale Dario Serpico,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统计算了核心坍缩超新星中Λ超子的产生速率，发现非轻子奇异数改变反应（尤其是NN↔NΛ散射）在10⁻¹¹-10⁻¹⁰秒量级即可实现Λ化学平衡，远快于原中子星宏观演化时间尺度；同时发现Λ超子可显著增强低能缪子中微子和反中微子的不透明度，可能影响缪子轻子数的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀奇异粒子通常被假设在致密物质状态方程中处于化学平衡，但建立这种平衡所需的弱相互作用时间尺度在核心坍缩超新星中尚未被系统评估，因此需要量化Λ超子的产生速率和平衡时间尺度。

Method: 在热密且同位旋不对称的原中子星条件下，计算了Λ超子的产生速率；采用受超核弱衰变数据约束的有效场论框架，超越了单纯的单介子交换描述。

Result: 非轻子反应（NN↔NΛ散射）主导Λ化学平衡，时间尺度10⁻¹¹-10⁻¹⁰秒；短程接触相互作用主导非轻子反应率；半轻子通道虽慢，但打开了额外的低能缪子中微子吸收通道（如ν_μ+Λ→μ⁻+p等），且Λ诱导的中微子不透明度超过了相应的核子贡献。

Conclusion: 支持Λ超子在所研究条件下处于局域化学平衡；为味相关的中微子输运、缪子化过程及原中子星演化提供了新的弱相互作用输入。

Abstract: Strange hadrons are commonly included in dense-matter equation-of-state models by imposing chemical equilibrium, but the weak-interaction timescales required to establish it in core-collapse supernovae have not been systematically assessed. In this paper we compute the $Λ$-hyperon production rates in the hot, dense, and isospin-asymmetric conditions characteristic of post-collapse proto-neutron stars. We find that local $Λ$ chemical equilibration is driven by nonleptonic strangeness-changing reactions, especially $NN\leftrightarrow NΛ$ scattering, on timescales of order $10^{-11}$-$10^{-10}$ s, many orders of magnitude shorter than macroscopic proto-neutron-star evolution timescales. Using an effective-field-theory framework constrained by hypernuclear weak-decay data, we find that short-range contact interactions dominate the nonleptonic rates, beyond a pure one-meson-exchange description. Semileptonic channels are too slow to set the equilibrium $Λ$ abundance, but they open additional absorption channels for low-energy muon neutrinos and antineutrinos, such as $ν_μ+Λ\toμ^-+p$ and $p+μ^-+\barν_μ\toΛ$. At low energies, these $Λ$-induced neutrino opacities exceed the corresponding nucleonic contributions for muon (anti)neutrinos, possibly influencing the evolution of the muon lepton number during proto-neutron-star deleptonization. These results support local chemical equilibrium for $Λ$ hyperons under the conditions studied and provide new weak-interaction input for flavor-dependent neutrino transport, muonization, and proto-neutron-star evolution.

</details>


### [165] [Light tetraquark states with $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ from QCD sum rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02109)
*Yi-Wei Jiang,Hua-Xing Chen,Er-Liang Cui,Ding-Kun Lian,Wen-Ying Liu,Niu Su*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD求和规则系统研究了光四夸克态（$J^{PC}=1^{--}$）的谱性质，构建了完整的局域流基组，覆盖六个味道-同位旋组态，计算了各通道的最低质量，并与先前$1^{-+}$四夸克和混合态结果进行比较，同时补全了先前缺失的等张量$1^{-+}$四夸克态质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究光四夸克态$1^{--}$的谱性质，提供区分四夸克和混合态构型的有效信息，并补全先前研究中缺失的等张量$1^{-+}$四夸克态质量数据。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则方法，在双夸克-反双夸克图像下构建完整的局域流算符集，投影到六个味道-同位旋组态（同位旋标量、同位旋矢量、同位旋张量），通过求和规则计算各通道的最低质量。

Result: 六个通道的最低质量分别为：$1.64^{+0.15}_{-0.14}$ GeV (等标$qq\bar q\bar q$), $1.86^{+0.14}_{-0.14}$ GeV ($qs\bar q\bar s$), $2.34^{+0.23}_{-0.30}$ GeV ($ss\bar s\bar s$), $1.53^{+0.17}_{-0.19}$ GeV (等矢$qq\bar q\bar q$), $1.86^{+0.14}_{-0.14}$ GeV (等矢$qs\bar q\bar s$), $2.24^{+0.12}_{-0.14}$ GeV (等张$qq\bar q\bar q$)。同时补全了等张量$1^{-+}$四夸克态质量$2.19^{+0.26}_{-0.24}$ GeV。

Conclusion: 获得的$1^{--}$四夸克谱与先前$1^{-+}$四夸克和混合态结果对比，可为光强子谱中区分四夸克和混合态构型提供有用信息。

Abstract: We perform a systematic QCD sum rule study of light tetraquark states with $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ in the diquark--antidiquark picture. A complete set of local interpolating currents is constructed and projected onto six flavor-isospin configurations ($q=u/d$): the isoscalar $q q\bar q\bar q$, $q s\bar q\bar s$, and $s s\bar s\bar s$ sectors, the isovector $q q\bar q\bar q$ and $q s\bar q\bar s$ sectors, and the isotensor $q q\bar q\bar q$ sector. The lowest masses in these sectors are derived to be $1.64^{+0.15}_{-0.14}$~GeV, $1.86^{+0.14}_{-0.14}$~GeV, $2.34^{+0.23}_{-0.30}$~GeV, $1.53^{+0.17}_{-0.19}$~GeV, $1.86^{+0.14}_{-0.14}$~GeV, and $2.24^{+0.12}_{-0.14}$~GeV, respectively. We further compare the present $1^{--}$ tetraquark spectrum with previous QCD sum rule results for the $1^{-+}$ tetraquark and hybrid states~\cite{Su:2025bhv}, aiming to provide useful information for distinguishing tetraquark and hybrid configurations in the light hadron spectrum. As an additional improvement, we complete the previously missing isotensor $1^{-+}$ tetraquark entry and obtain its lowest mass to be $M=2.19^{+0.26}_{-0.24}~\mathrm{GeV}$, which is included in the spectral comparison.

</details>


### [166] [Searching for single production of a vector-like $Y$ quark decaying into $bW$ at the FCC-eh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02177)
*Liangliang Shang,Weifan Zhu,Bingfang Yang,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在FCC-eh对撞机上，通过电弱过程单产生矢量类Y夸克（电荷Q=-4/3，衰变Y→bW）的排除与发现潜力，分析了电子束极化P_e=-80%下三个质心能量（3.46、5.29、6.9 TeV），综合考虑W玻色子的轻子和强子衰变通道，通过详细探测器模拟给出了g*—m_Y参数空间中的2σ排除限和5σ发现灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理信号，具体研究一种带有奇异电荷（-4/3）的矢量类Y夸克在未来的FCC-eh对撞机上的单产生可能性。

Method: 基于FCC-eh对撞机环境，在电子束极化P_e=-80%下，选取三个质心能量（3.46、5.29、6.9 TeV），对Y→bW过程进行详细探测器模拟和事件分析，分别处理W玻色子的轻子和强子衰变通道（强子通道中重建W-jet），利用运动学观测量压制标准模型本底。

Result: 给出了g*—m_Y平面上的2σ排除限和5σ发现灵敏度；发现强子衰变通道提供更强的排除和发现灵敏度，且灵敏度随质心能量提高而增强。

Conclusion: FCC-eh对撞机对矢量类Y夸克（Q=-4/3）具有显著的探测潜力，强子通道优于轻子通道，更高能量有助于提升灵敏度。

Abstract: We investigate the exclusion and discovery potential for single production of a vector-like $Y$ quark with electric charge $Q=-4/3$, followed by the decay $Y\to bW$, at the FCC-eh. The $Y$ quark is allowed to couple to both first- and third-generation down-type quarks. The analysis is performed for an electron-beam polarization of $P_e=-80\%$ at $\sqrt{s}=3.46$, $5.29$, and $6.9~\mathrm{TeV}$. Both leptonic and hadronic $W$-boson decay channels are considered. In the hadronic channel, the boosted $W$-boson is reconstructed as a $W$-jet, and kinematic observables are used to suppress the Standard Model (SM) backgrounds. By performing a detailed detector simulations and event analysis, we present the $2σ$ exclusion limits and $5σ$ discovery reaches in the $g^*$--$m_Y$ plane, where $g^*$ is $Y$ coupling strength to the SM quarks. We find that the hadronic channel can provide stronger exclusion and discovery sensitivities, which are improved with increasing $\sqrt{s}$ at the FCC-eh.

</details>


### [167] [Sideband Structure of Axion Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02232)
*Run-Min Yao,Xiao-Jun Bi,Peng-Fei Yin,Qing-Guo Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文发展了周期性轴子背景下的线性化麦克斯韦-轴子系统Floquet-Bloch边带理论，揭示了光子-轴子边带梯级结构，统一了不同参数共振机制，并分析了泵浦场驱动的模式混合与不稳定性拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在周期轴子背景中研究光子-轴子系统的线性化动力学，特别是泵浦场诱导的边带生成、模式简并与稳定性问题。

Method: 采用Floquet-Bloch边带形式对线性化麦克斯韦-轴子系统进行分析，结合Krein符号、Mathieu方程比较、射线投影WKB描述、外场费曼图以及时域单值性计算等多种理论工具。

Result: 揭示了孤立折叠简并附近的两模交叉结构由碰撞模式的辛符号决定；同Krein符号碰撞产生稳定避让交叉，异号碰撞产生参数不稳定性，统一了轴子-光子差通道与Mathieu及Masaki-Aoki-Soda共振；固定频率传输中通量信号可区分前向转换、前后向阻带和分布反射；射线投影给出WKB驱动前向混合描述，有效波数区别于真轴子动量；外场图再现边带选择定则，时域单值性计算验证了不稳定性拓扑和有限耦合位移。

Conclusion: 该形式框架统一了多种轴子-光子共振机制，提供了从边带结构到不稳定性拓扑的完整理论描述，为轴子-光子混合系统的分析奠定了系统的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a Floquet--Bloch sideband formulation of the linearized Maxwell--axion system in a coherent periodic axion background. Linearizing around prescribed magnetic and axion fields, we show that the pump generates a sideband ladder of photon and axion branches. Near an isolated folded degeneracy, this ladder reduces to a two-mode crossing whose algebra is fixed by the symplectic signatures of the colliding modes. In temporal fixed-momentum evolution, same-Krein-sign collisions give stable avoided crossings, whereas opposite-sign collisions give parametric instabilities, unifying the axion-photon difference channel with the Mathieu and Masaki-Aoki-Soda resonances. In stationary fixed-frequency transfer, the corresponding flux signatures distinguish bounded forward conversion from forward-backward stop bands and distributed reflection. Ray projection of a temporal pump gives a related but local WKB description of driven forward mixing, with an effective wavenumber distinct from the true axion momentum. External-field diagrams reproduce the sideband selection rules, and full temporal monodromy calculations verify the instability topology and finite-coupling shifts.

</details>


### [168] [A Comprehensive Analysis of $B_s \to D_s^{**}\ellν_\ell$ Decays Within and Beyond the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02276)
*Karthik Jain,Tarun Kumar,Barilang Mawlong,Shantanu Sahoo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型及新物理框架下，研究B_s → D_s^{**} ℓ ν_ℓ 稀有半轻子衰变，基于重夸克有效理论（包括α_s和Λ/m_Q修正）计算形状因子，利用数据驱动方法提取HQET参数并通过z-展开参数化，预测精确的轻子普适性比率R_{D_s^{**}}，并在弱有效理论、SMEFT及一般双希格斯二重态模型下分析新物理效应，发现标量和张量算符可导致部分观测量偏离标准模型超过2σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究B_s介子到激发态D_s^{**}介子的半轻子衰变过程，在标准模型和超出标准模型的新物理场景下精确计算可观测量，并检验轻子普适性。

Method: 基于重夸克有效理论（HQET）计算形状因子（包含α_s和Λ/m_Q阶修正）；采用数据驱动方法提取HQET参数；利用z-展开技术在全运动学区域参数化形状因子；通过双角度衰变角分布计算可观测量；在WET、SMEFT和一般2HDM框架下进行全局实数和复数Wilson系数分析。

Result: 给出了四个通道的轻子普适性比率精确预测：R_{D_{s0}^*}=0.158(20), R_{D_{s1}^*}=0.045(5), R_{D_{s1}}=0.073(4), R_{D_{s2}^*}=0.066(9)；标量和张量新物理算符可导致部分可观测量与标准模型偏差超过2σ。

Conclusion: 本文提供了B_s → D_s^{**}半轻子衰变的精确理论预测，并证明了这些衰变过程在探测新物理（尤其是标量和张量型新物理）方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: We examine the exclusive semileptonic decays $B_s \to D_s^{**} \ell ν_\ell$, with $D_s^{**} =$ $\bigl\{D_{s0}^*,D_{s1}^*,D_{s1},D_{s2}^*\bigr\}$, within the Standard Model and beyond, using form factors evaluated in the Heavy Quark Effective Theory, including corrections up to $\mathcal{O}(α_s, Λ/{m_Q})$. A data-driven approach is employed to extract Heavy Quark Effective Theory parameters, and the resulting synthetic data are used to parameterize the form factors via the $z$-expansion. With the resulting form factor information across the full kinematic region, we compute various observables derived from the two-fold angular decay distribution, and predict precise lepton flavor universality ratios: $R_{D_{s0}^*}= 0.158(20)$, $R_{D_{s1}^*}= 0.045(5)$, $R_{D_{s1}}= 0.073(4)$, $R_{D_{s2}^*} = 0.066(9)$. We also analyse potential new physics effects using the Weak Effective Theory and the Standard Model Effective Field Theory, performing a global analysis considering both real and complex Wilson coefficients. Furthermore, we investigate new physics contributions arising from the general Two Higgs Doublet Model. We evaluate the sensitivity of decay observables to new physics, highlighting their potential to probe deviations from the Standard Model in future measurements. Notably, the scalar and tensor new physics operators induce large sensitivity, with some observables deviating by more than $2 σ$ from Standard Model predictions.

</details>


### [169] [A Maximum-Entropy Method for Zero-Skewness Valence GPDs Constrained by Nucleon Electromagnetic Form Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02302)
*Seung-il Nam*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降维最大熵方法框架，利用四个核子电磁形状因子和价夸克部分子分布函数等互补约束，构建无偏斜价夸克广义部分子分布函数的横向轮廓，通过正定轮廓函数和熵正则化在低维流形上实现数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束仅通过形状因子和规则固定GPD的x积分矩，而向前极限和反常磁矩分别约束H_v^q和E_v^q的归一化，缺乏将弹性形状因子约束与x依赖的横向结构直接关联的系统方法。

Method: 采用降维最大熵方法，通过ansatz H_v^q(x,t)=q_v(x)exp[t f_H^q(x)]和E_v^q(x,t)=e_v^q(x)exp[t f_E^q(x)]引入正定轮廓函数，利用熵泛函作为正则化准则在低维正定轮廓流形上构建解，并采用约化形式f(x)=0.05+(1-x)^2 exp(c_0+c_1 x+c_2 x^2)抑制弹性矩无法约束的局部模式。

Result: 数值验证表明，该方法重现了所施加的弹性矩约束，通过离散网格归一化满足向前极限归一化条件，并给出了大x区域预期的横向收缩冲击参数分布。

Conclusion: 该框架为连接弹性形状因子约束与x依赖横向轮廓提供了受控的无偏斜基线，并为未来纳入经验形状因子拟合、现代PDF输入、格点QCD广义形状因子及硬排他观测量等分析提供了稳定的起点。

Abstract: We formulate a reduced-profile maximum-entropy method (MEM) framework for constructing constrained zero-skewness valence-quark generalized parton distribution (GPD) transverse profiles from the four nucleon electromagnetic form factors $F_1^p(t)$, $F_1^n(t)$, $F_2^p(t)$, and $F_2^n(t)$. The form-factor sum rules fix only $x$-integrated moments of the GPDs; the forward limit of $H_v^q$ is fixed separately by the valence parton distribution functions, and the normalization of $E_v^q$ by the flavor anomalous magnetic moments. These complementary constraints are combined through the ansatz $H_v^q(x,t)=q_v(x)\exp[t f_H^q(x)]$ and $E_v^q(x,t)=e_v^q(x)\exp[t f_E^q(x)]$, where the positive profile functions encode the $x$-dependent transverse structure. Rather than attempting an unrestricted functional inversion, we use the entropy functional as a regularizing criterion on a low-dimensional positive profile manifold. In the numerical proof-of-concept calculation, a smooth elastic form-factor input and analytic forward distributions are adopted, together with the reduced form $f(x)=0.05+(1-x)^2\exp(c_0+c_1x+c_2x^2)$, which suppresses local modes that elastic moments alone cannot constrain. Within this reduced ansatz, the resulting profiles reproduce the imposed elastic moment constraints, satisfy the forward normalizations after discrete-grid normalization, and give impact-parameter distributions with the expected transverse shrinkage at large $x$. The construction provides a controlled zero-skewness baseline for connecting elastic form-factor constraints to $x$-dependent transverse profiles, and it offers a stable starting point for future analyses incorporating empirical form-factor fits, modern PDF inputs, lattice-QCD generalized form factors, and hard exclusive observables.

</details>


### [170] [New constraints on non-unitary neutrino mixing from 8 years of IceCube DeepCore atmospheric neutrino data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02355)
*Sharmistha Chattopadhyay,Anil Kumar,Sanjib Kumar Agarwalla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用IceCube DeepCore 8年大气中微子数据，对非幺正中微子混合参数进行了最严格的约束，其中α₃₃ > -0.027（90% CL），结果与标准幺正混合框架一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准中微子混合由3×3幺正矩阵描述，但额外重惰性中微子可能导致非幺正混合。大气中微子因其基线范围和能量跨度广，是探测这类效应的理想探针。

Method: 使用IceCube DeepCore 8年公开大气中微子数据（高纯度ν_μ CC样本），利用地球物质效应对中微子振荡的影响，分析非幺正混合参数，特别关注ν_μ→ν_μ通道中的领头阶非幺正效应。

Result: 数据与标准幺正混合框架一致，未发现显著偏差。对非幺正参数α₃₃给出了迄今最严格的约束（α₃₃ > -0.027，90% CL），其他非幺正参数也获得了具有竞争力的约束水平。

Conclusion: IceCube DeepCore大气中微子数据对非幺正混合参数具有强灵敏度和约束能力，证实了标准幺正混合框架的合理性。

Abstract: The mixing between flavor and mass eigenstates of active neutrinos is described by a $3\times3$ unitary matrix. However, the presence of additional heavy sterile neutrino states can lead to a non-unitary neutrino mixing scenario. Atmospheric neutrinos, with their wide range of baselines and energies, provide an excellent probe of such effects. In particular, Earth matter effects in neutrino oscillations play an important role, as the neutral-current potential contributes non-trivially in the presence of non-unitarity. In this work, we use 8 years of publicly available atmospheric neutrino data of IceCube DeepCore to probe this non-unitary neutrino mixing scenario. This high-purity $ν_μ$ CC sample provides strong sensitivity, especially to the non-unitary parameters appearing at leading order in the $ν_μ\rightarrow ν_μ$ channel. The data sample is found to be consistent with the standard unitary mixing framework with no significant deviation. Using this data sample, we place the most stringent bound to date of $α_{33} > -0.027$ at 90% CL, while the other non-unitary parameters are constrained at competitive levels.

</details>


### [171] [Symmetry Analysis of Compact Tetraquark States and Implications for the Level Ordering of the Fully Charmed Candidates $X(6600)$, $X(6900)$, and $X(7100)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02382)
*Shuai Yin,Ti Gong,Jingya Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过群论方法研究紧致四夸克态的自旋-宇称分布，发现低能态倾向于J^P=2^+，且对称性约束主导能谱特征，对全粲四夸克候选态X(6600)、X(6900)、X(7100)的能级定位及超出CMI动力学的机制有重要启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期实验观测（如全粲四夸克候选态）的启发，旨在从对称性约束出发系统研究低能紧致四夸克态的J^P分布规律，探究其内在节点结构及主导能谱的物理机制。

Method: 假设两夸克和两反夸克呈四面体或正方形排布，利用置换群S_4到S_2×S_2的约化表示，从qqqq系统推导qqq̄q̄系统的固有节点结构（轨道角动量L≤3），并在此框架下确定可达J^P态的分布。同时纳入色磁相互作用（CMI）效应进行对比分析。

Result: 1) 低能紧致四夸克态倾向于J^P=2^+；2) 对称性诱导的J^P分布与三味四夸克系统高度相似；3) 分布峰值位置在引入CMI效应后保持不变；4) 全粲四夸克候选态X(6600)、X(6900)、X(7100)可能位于全粲四夸克谱的相对低能级；5) 暗示存在超出CMI的动力学的机制参与竞争或抵消。

Conclusion: 低能紧致四夸克态的主要谱学特征由对称性约束主导，而非动力学细节；对称性分类在奇异强子谱学中具有鲁棒性；全粲四夸克态需考虑超越CMI的动力学机制。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experimental observations, we investigate the $J^P$ distribution of low-energy compact tetraquark states. Assuming that two quarks and two antiquarks are arranged in either a tetrahedral or a square configuration, we employ the restricted representations of the permutation group $S_4$ onto $S_2 \times S_2$ to derive the inherent nodal structure of the $qq\bar q\bar q$ system from that of the $qqqq$ system for orbital angular momentum $L \leq 3$. Based on this framework, we determine the distribution of accessible $J^P$ states and find that low-energy compact tetraquark states are likely to favor $J^P=2^+$. Our analysis yields two observations that further support the dynamical robustness of symmetry-based classifications in exotic hadron spectroscopy. First, the symmetry-induced $J^P$ distribution of compact tetraquark states closely resembles that obtained for the three-flavor four-quark system. Second, the location of the distribution peak remains unchanged when chromomagnetic interaction (CMI) effects are incorporated. Together, these results suggest that the dominant features of the low-lying spectrum are governed primarily by symmetry constraints rather than by the details of the underlying dynamics. These findings further imply that the fully charmed tetraquark candidates $X(6600)$, $X(6900)$, and $X(7100)$ may occupy relatively low-lying levels of the fully charmed tetraquark spectrum. They also indicate that mechanisms beyond CMI dynamics are likely involved, potentially mitigating or competing with the effects of CMI in compact fully charmed tetraquark states.

</details>


### [172] [Chebyshev Approximations of Feynman Integrals for Collider Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02411)
*Samuel Abreu,Afonso Guerreiro,Ben Page*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于切比雪夫多项式近似的Feynman积分正则微分方程求解新方法，通过自适应采样优化收敛，在双精度浮点运算下实现，并在双圈五点多例中证明了其与最先进的一重积分方法竞争力相当，且几乎无需针对假奇异点进行人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Feynman积分求解方法在处理假奇异点时需要大量人工干预，且常规数值方法效率有限，亟需一种自动化、高效且鲁棒的求解方案。

Method: 利用Feynman积分的解析性，采用切比雪夫多项式沿路径构造快速收敛的多项式逼近；引入自适应采样方法动态优化收敛过程；在双精度浮点运算框架下实现该算法。

Result: 在双圈、五点的物理相空间系列算例中验证了稳定性；与最先进的一重积分方法相比具有竞争力；几乎无需针对不同案例进行人工干预以处理假奇异点。

Conclusion: 该切比雪夫逼近方法为Feynman积分正则微分方程的数值求解提供了一种高效、自动化且鲁棒的新途径，有望降低人工调参成本并提升计算效率。

Abstract: We present a novel approach for solving canonical differential equations for Feynman integrals based on an approximation of the integrals with Chebyshev polynomials. By exploiting the analyticity properties of Feynman integrals, the method constructs rapidly converging polynomial approximations along a path, enabling highly efficient numerical evaluation. Moreover, we introduce an adaptive approximation method that dynamically samples to optimise convergence. We implement this framework in double-precision arithmetic and demonstrate its stability across physical phase space using a series of two-loop, five-point examples. Our proof-of-principle implementation proves competitive with state-of-the-art one-fold integral methods, while requiring little to no case-by-case intervention to handle spurious singularities.

</details>


### [173] [Event-axis TMD measurements in $e^+e^-$ and SIDIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02438)
*Daniel Diaz Fernandez,Patricia Andrea Gutierrez Garcia,Ignazio Scimemi,Wouter Waalewijn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在e⁺e⁻碰撞中通过推力轴测量强子来提取横动量依赖（TMD）碎裂函数，完成了软因子的算符级表述和单圈检验，扩展到SIDIS中的1-喷注度因子化，提出了包含事件形状依赖和非微扰关联的模型，并在artemide中实现了对数重求和。通过Pythia8.3模拟数据验证，发现模型能灵活描述数据，参数量级符合Λ_QCD/Q预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统背对背强子对测量需要解耦两个TMD碎裂函数，而基于推力轴的测量只需一个TMD碎裂函数，提供了一种互补且更简洁的提取途径。

Method: 基于已有因子化定理，完善软因子的算符级表述；使用δ-调节器进行单圈检验；将结果扩展到SIDIS中1-喷注度因子化；提出包含事件形状依赖和q_T-事件形状关联的非微扰模型；在artemide中实现q_T和推力对数重求和；使用Pythia8.3模拟数据进行拟合验证。

Result: 非微扰模型能灵活描述Pythia模拟数据，拟合参数量级符合Λ_QCD/Q的预期；q_T/(τQ)对数的重求和对方程质量影响较小，但改变了拟合参数值。

Conclusion: 基于推力轴的TMD碎裂函数测量是可行的互补方案，所提出的非微扰模型及重求和方案在模拟数据中表现良好，为今后从实验数据中提取TMD碎裂函数奠定了理论基础和计算工具。

Abstract: Transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) fragmentation in $e^+e^-$ collisions can be studied by measuring hadrons with respect to the thrust axis, and has been measured at Belle. This provides a complementary way to extract TMD fragmentation functions, avoiding the need to disentangle the two TMD fragmentation functions that enter conventional back-to-back hadron-pair measurements. Starting from the established factorization theorems for this observable, we complete the operator-level formulation of the soft ingredients and perform one-loop checks using the $δ$-regulator. We also extend existing results for 1-jettiness factorization in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering (SIDIS), where analogous measurements give access to the TMD parton distribution functions of the incoming hadron. For phenomenology, we discuss the nonperturbative effects and propose a model that captures both the event-shape dependence and correlations between the event-shape and transverse-momentum measurements. We resum the transverse-momentum and thrust logarithms, explore several schemes for treating the latter, and implement it in artemide. As a first validation, we compare to simulated $e^+e^-$ data from Pythia8.3. We find that the proposed nonperturbative model is flexible enough to describe the simulated data, with fitted parameters of the expected size in powers of $Λ_{\rm QCD}/Q$. In this test, the resummation of the logarithms of $q_T/(τQ)$ appears to have little impact on the fit quality, but changes the fit parameters.

</details>


### [174] [Transverse-spin dependent energy-energy correlators in proton-proton collisions within the dihadron fragmentation framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02441)
*Zhong-Bo Kang,Andreas Metz,Daniel Pitonyak,Congyue Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了横向极化质子-质子碰撞中喷注内两强子能量-能量关联，基于双强子碎裂函数和横向性部分子分布函数的全局QCD分析模型进行数值预测，结果与STAR实验测量高度吻合，并发现大横向动量数据更偏好与格点QCD核子张量荷一致的横向性提取，为近端能量-能量关联子的非微扰机制及探测核子横向自旋效应提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索横向极化质子-质子碰撞中喷注内能量-能量关联的可观测性，验证其作为探测核子内横向自旋效应的有效探针，并检验现有全局QCD分析模型的预测能力。

Method: 基于双强子碎裂函数和横向性部分子分布函数的全局QCD分析，构建简单模型计算喷注内两强子能量-能量关联，并与STAR实验数据对比。

Result: 数值预测与STAR测量结果高度一致；大喷注横向动量数据更偏好与格点QCD张量荷一致的横向性提取。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了近端能量-能量关联子的非微扰机制，并凸显了该类观测量在探测核子横向自旋效应方面的潜力。

Abstract: We calculate energy-energy correlations for two hadrons produced inside a jet in transversely polarized proton-proton collisions. We make numerical predictions based on a simple model that utilizes a previous global QCD analysis of dihadron fragmentation and transversity parton distribution functions. The results show remarkable agreement with a very recent STAR measurement. We also find the data at large jet transverse momentum have a slight preference for extractions of transversity that are consistent with lattice QCD computations of the nucleon tensor charges. Overall, this work provides further evidence for the underlying non-perturbative mechanism of near-side energy-energy correlators as well as highlights the potential for these observables to probe transverse-spin effects inside the nucleon.

</details>


### [175] [The $D_{s1}(2460)$ and other open-charm $1^+$ states in relativistic chiral effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02452)
*Ze-Rui Liang,Qi-Chao Xiao,Zhi-Hui Guo,De-Liang Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在相对论性 U(3) 手征有效场论中推导了粲矢量介子与轻赝标量玻色子相互作用的手征势，计算了 S 波和 P 波散射长度，验证了重夸克自旋对称性对低能常数的估计。研究发现 D_{s1}(2460) 为束缚态极点，D_1(2430) 由两个极点构成，并揭示其群论起源与非 \bar{q}q 性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推导粲矢量介子与轻赝标量玻色子相互作用的完整手征势，并在重夸克自旋对称性框架下验证低能常数，以理解相关共振态的结构和性质。

Method: 基于相对论性 U(3) 手征有效场论，计算到次领头阶的粲矢量介子-赝标量介子手征势，通过散射长度计算和极点分析研究共振态结构，并利用大 N_C 极限鉴别态的性质。

Result: S 波 I=1/2 D^*π 散射长度与格点 QCD 数据吻合；D_{s1}(2460) 为束缚态极点；D_1(2430) 对应第二和第三黎曼面上的两个极点；低极点与 D_{s1}(2460) 同属 SU(3) 三重态，高极点属六重态；所有态在大 N_C 极限下流向复无穷，非 \bar{q}q 态。

Conclusion: 本文提供了粲矢量介子-赝标量介子相互作用的量化基准，为未来格点 QCD 和费米子对撞研究提供了理论基础，并澄清了 D_{s1}(2460) 和 D_1(2430) 的极点结构与群论归属。

Abstract: We derive the pertinent chiral potentials for charmed vector meson interactions with light pseudoscalar bosons in a relativistic U(3) chiral effective field theory up to next-to-leading order. Predictions for the $S$- and $P$-wave scattering lengths are obtained for all the relevant elastic channels. A comparison with the most recent -- and currently the sole -- lattice QCD data on the $S$-wave $I=1/2$ $D^\astπ$ scattering length at a pion mass of $391$~MeV reveals good agreement, thereby validating the estimation of low energy constants via heavy quark spin symmetry. Within the relativistic formalism, we confirm that the $D_{s1}(2460)$ can be identified with a bound state pole, while the $D_1(2430)$ corresponds to the interplay of two poles: a lower one on the second Riemann sheet and a higher one on the third Riemann sheet. We show that the $D_{s1}(2460)$ and the lower $D_1(2430)$ pole originate from the same flavor SU(3) triplet, whereas the higher $D_1(2430)$ pole belongs to the SU(3) sextet. All these states are not of $\bar{q}q$ nature, as they flow to complex infinity in the large $N_C$ limit. Our results provide quantitative benchmarks for future lattice QCD and femtoscopic studies.

</details>


### [176] [Identifying $Σ(1380)$ and $Σ(1430)$ in the $J/ψ\to Λπ\barΣ$ reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02485)
*Wen-Tao Lyu,Eulogio Oset,De-Min Li,En Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过研究 J/ψ→ΛπΣ̄ 反应中 π⁺Λ 的低能质量分布，寻找低能Σ⁺态的信号。除了清晰的 Σ(1385)(3/2⁺) 信号外，还发现了已被Belle合作组确认的预测态 Σ(1430)(1/2⁻) 的较小峰值。仅考虑 πΛ 相互作用时，包含 Σ(1430) 和 Σ(1380)(1/2⁻) 能更好拟合低能谱；但考虑 πΣ̄ 相互作用后，Σ(1380) 不再需要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 J/ψ→ΛπΣ̄ 反应的 π⁺Λ 低能质量分布中寻找低能Σ⁺重子态的信号，验证理论预测的奇特重子态是否存在。

Method: 分析 J/ψ→ΛπΣ̄ 衰变中 π⁺Λ 质量谱的低能区域，分别考虑仅包含 πΛ 相互作用以及包含 πΣ̄ 相互作用的两种分析方案，对比拟合效果。

Result: 确认了 Σ(1385)(3/2⁺) 的清晰信号和 Σ(1430)(1/2⁻) 的较小峰值。仅 πΛ 相互作用时需引入 Σ(1380)(1/2⁻) 才能较好拟合低能谱；但引入 πΣ̄ 相互作用后，Σ(1380) 不再是必要的。

Conclusion: Σ(1430)态得到进一步确认，而Σ(1380)态的存在性依赖于是否考虑πΣ̄相互作用通道，暗示该态可能不是独立存在的共振态，而是由通道耦合效应产生的动力学结构。

Abstract: We study the $J/ψ\to Λπ\barΣ$ reaction by looking at the $π^+ Λ$ mass distribution at low energies, in search of signals for the low lying $Σ^+$ states. Apart from a clear signal of the $Σ(1385) (3/2^+)$ state, we find a smaller peak for the predicted $Σ(1430) (1/2^-)$, which has already been confirmed by the Belle Collaboration. A first analysis, considering only the $πΛ$ interaction, shows that the low energy part of the spectrum is better reproduced including contributions from the $Σ(1430)$ and the predicted $Σ(1380)(1/2^-)$ state that has been claimed before from analyses of different experiments. However, when we consider the $π\barΣ$ interaction the need for the $Σ(1380)$ disappears.

</details>


### [177] [A critical look at low-scale cosmological phase transitions in the PTA era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02505)
*Simone Biondini,Philipp Schicho*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对暗阿贝尔希格斯 sector 中的低能标相变进行了精确研究，发现当前 PTA 观测偏好的参数区域接近有效场论的边界，高维算符变得重要，且即使在受控区域内，预测的引力波信号仍不被 PTA 数据支持。同时，研究还揭示了不对称冻结-out 暗物质机制与强相变所偏好的规范耦合自然兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列合作组最近报告了随机引力波背景的初步证据，这激发了对低能标宇宙学相变及其引力波信号的精确研究需求，以检验理论预测与观测数据的一致性。

Method: 采用维度约化的高温有效场论方法，系统量化了热重求和、高阶匹配修正和高维算符对相变热力学和引力波信号的影响，同时分析了暗区和可见区的热力学与流体动力学耦合/退耦情况。

Result: PTA 数据偏好的参数区域位于有效场论有效性边界附近，高维算符效应显著；即便在可控区域内，经高阶热修正后预测的引力波信号仍不被 PTA 数据支持；不对称冻结-out 暗物质机制可与强相变偏好的规范耦合自然兼容。

Conclusion: 研究强调了系统可控的有限温度计算对于从低能标宇宙学相变获得可靠引力波预言的重要性，表明当前 PTA 观测对暗阿贝尔希格斯相变场景构成压力。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent evidence for a stochastic gravitational-wave (GW) background reported by pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations, we perform a precision study of low-scale phase transitions in a dark Abelian Higgs sector, a minimal gauge theory of spontaneous symmetry breaking relevant for cosmological phase transitions. Using dimensionally reduced high-temperature effective field theory, we quantify the impact of thermal resummation, higher-order matching corrections, and higher-dimensional operators on the phase-transition thermodynamics and the resulting GW signal. We find that the parameter region favored by current PTA observations lies close to the boundary of validity of the effective field theory, where higher-dimensional operators become increasingly important. Even within this controlled region, the predicted signal remains disfavored by the PTA data, despite the substantial shifts induced by higher-order thermal corrections. We further delineate parameter regions where the dark and visible sectors are thermally and hydrodynamically coupled or decoupled, and revisit the dark matter phenomenology, identifying asymmetric freeze-out as naturally compatible with both the observed relic abundance and the gauge couplings favored by strong phase transitions. Our results underscore the importance of systematically controlled finite-temperature calculations for reliable GW predictions from low-scale cosmological phase transitions.

</details>
